# TAREL code map

Every module in `src/tarel`, described as input, processing and output (EVA: Eingabe,
Verarbeitung, Ausgabe). Generated by `tools/generate_code_map.py` from docstrings,
signatures and calls; do not edit by hand.

How to read an entry:

- **Input** lists what callers pass in and which sources the module reads.
- **Processing** lists the public functions, methods and classes with their purpose.
- **Output** lists return types and the effects on state, files, terminal or network.
- **Uses** and **Used by** name the TAREL modules it imports and that import it.

## Packages

| Package | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `tarel.annotations` | Provider-neutral annotation tasks, proposals, and execution. |
| `tarel.bundle` | Offline install bundles: TAREL, chosen hub packages, and their wheels for one target machine. |
| `tarel.cli` | The ``tarel`` command line. |
| `tarel.connectors` | Connector contracts and the explicit connector host. |
| `tarel.context` | Context packets: the bounded, hashed slice of a graph that a harness hands to a model. |
| `tarel.discovery` | Optional, resumable discovery runs for coding agents. |
| `tarel.entity_resolution` | Auditable entity-resolution candidates and graph projections. |
| `tarel.expansion` | Optional private-handle context expansion. |
| `tarel.focus` | Persisted demand-driven slices across graphs and lineage. |
| `tarel.graph` | Local technical and semantic graph contracts. |
| `tarel.hub` | Discovery, inspection, project suggestion, and explicit installation for TAREL Hub. |
| `tarel.knowledge` | Small file-first knowledge attachments for annotation context. |
| `tarel.lineage` | Static workflow and table-lineage contracts. |
| `tarel.logical_joins` | Optional reviewable logical joins; never physical graph edges or query executors. |
| `tarel.object_bindings` | Optional, metadata-only object-to-value bindings. |
| `tarel.object_families` | Experimental public object-family declarations; no query execution. |
| `tarel.packages` | Portable, dependency-free TAREL state packages. |
| `tarel.providers` | Optional structured-generation providers used by annotation runners. |
| `tarel.reference_mapping` | Auditable, directed reference-mapping candidates. |
| `tarel.relationships` | Relationship candidates and human decisions. |
| `tarel.retrieval` | Optional local retrieval over safe graph-derived documents. |
| `tarel.sdk` | Small embedded Python surface over TAREL application use cases. |
| `tarel.semantic_concepts` | Experimental metadata concepts and hierarchies; no automatic equivalence. |
| `tarel.semantics` | Semantic-model imports kept separate from TAREL-authored annotations. |
| `tarel.sources` | Private logical source registry for connector-backed graph discovery. |
| `tarel.sync` | One-way publication of approved graph metadata from sites to a central view. |
| `tarel.topology` | Experimental, graph-bound logical topology contracts. |
| `tarel.ui` | Optional dependency-free local browser interface. |
| `tarel.workspaces` | Organizational views across one or more TAREL graphs. |

## `tarel.annotations`

### `src/tarel/annotations/application.py`

Annotation use cases: plan tasks, apply proposals, run provider batches and review.

- **Input:** parameters `config_path: Path | None`, `envelope: AnnotationProposalEnvelope`, `focus_name: str`, `include_fields: bool`, `include_small_domain_values: bool`, `knowledge_document_ids: tuple[str, ...]`, `knowledge_mode: str`, `knowledge_workspace: str | None`, `limit: int | None`, `max_errors: int | None`, `max_knowledge_characters: int`, `missing_only: bool` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `AnnotationApplyResult`: record with fields graph, path, target_id.
    - `AnnotationBatchResult`: record with fields graph, path, run.
    - `AnnotationReviewResult`: record with fields graph, path, records.
    - `AnnotationReviewResult.record`: record; returns `AnnotationReviewRecord`.
    - `plan_annotations_use_case`: plan annotations (use case); returns `tuple[AnnotationTask, ...]`. Uses the graph store.
    - `plan_focus_annotations_use_case`: plan focus annotations (use case); returns `tuple[AnnotationTask, ...]`. Calls `load_current_focus`, `graph_object_ids`; raises `AnnotationFailure`.
    - `apply_annotation_use_case`: apply annotation (use case); returns `AnnotationApplyResult`. Calls `validate_knowledge_references`, `apply_annotation_proposal`; uses the graph store.
    - `show_annotation_use_case`: show annotation (use case); returns `AnnotationReviewRecord`. Calls `annotation_review_record`; uses the graph store.
    - `list_annotation_reviews_use_case`: list annotation reviews (use case); returns `tuple[AnnotationReviewRecord, ...]`. Calls `list_annotation_reviews`; uses the graph store.
    - `edit_annotation_use_case`: edit annotation (use case); returns `AnnotationReviewResult`. Calls `edit_annotation`; uses the graph store.
    - `decide_annotation_use_case`: decide annotation (use case); returns `AnnotationReviewResult`. Calls `decide_annotation_scope`; uses the graph store.
    - `run_annotation_batch_use_case`: run annotation batch (use case); returns `AnnotationBatchResult`. Calls `load_provider`, `run_annotation_batch`; uses the graph store.
    - `save_provider_annotation`: save provider annotation; returns `GraphDocument`. Calls `apply_annotation_proposal`.
- **Output:** returns `AnnotationApplyResult`, `AnnotationBatchResult`, `AnnotationReviewRecord`, `AnnotationReviewResult`, `GraphDocument`, `tuple[AnnotationReviewRecord, ...]`, `tuple[AnnotationTask, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.apply`, `tarel.annotations.contracts`, `tarel.annotations.review`, `tarel.annotations.runner`, `tarel.annotations.tasks`, `tarel.connectors.application`, `tarel.connectors.contracts`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.focus.core`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.store`, `tarel.knowledge.application`, `tarel.knowledge.contracts`, `tarel.providers.host`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.annotation`, `tarel.cli.graph`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.sdk.annotation`, `tarel.ui.backend`

### `src/tarel/annotations/apply.py`

- **Input:** parameters `context_documents: tuple[KnowledgeReference, ...] | None`, `envelope: AnnotationProposalEnvelope`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `model: str | None`, `provider: str | None`, `source: str`
- **Processing:**

- **Processing:**

    - `AnnotationProposalEnvelope`: record with fields task_id, target_id, annotation, context_documents, mode, field_names, include_object.
    - `AnnotationProposalEnvelope.from_dict`: from dict; returns `AnnotationProposalEnvelope`.
    - `AnnotationRunResult`: record with fields planned, annotated, failed.
    - `AnnotationRunResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, int]`.
- **Output:** returns `AnnotationProposalEnvelope`, `ObjectAnnotationProposal`, `dict[str, int]`, `dict[str, object]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.knowledge.contracts`, `tarel.providers.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.annotations.apply`, `tarel.annotations.review`, `tarel.annotations.runner`, `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.annotations.tasks`, `tarel.cli._output`, `tarel.cli.annotation`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.knowledge.application`, `tarel.sdk.annotation`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.server`

Create deterministic annotation tasks from technical graph objects.

- **Input:** parameters `field_names: tuple[str, ...]`, `fields_by_target: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]]`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `include_object: bool`, `include_object_targets: frozenset[str]`, `knowledge_by_target: dict[str, KnowledgeContext] | None`, `limit: int | None`, `missing_only: bool`, `mode: str`, `namespace: str | None`, `node: GraphNode`, `objects: set[str] | None` …

## `tarel`

### `src/tarel/atomic.py`

Crash-safe replacement of state files.

- **Input:** parameters `directory: Path`, `durable: bool`, `lock_root: Path | None`, `path: Path`, `prefix: str`, `text: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `sync_directory`: Make a finished rename survive power loss. Windows has no such call for directories.
    - `write_atomic`: Replace ``path`` with ``text`` atomically. Calls `state_write_lock`.
- **Output:** deletes files; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.file_lock`
- **Used by:** `tarel.demo`, `tarel.discovery.store`, `tarel.entity_resolution.store`, `tarel.focus.store`, `tarel.graph.change_store`, `tarel.graph.selective`, `tarel.graph.store`, `tarel.knowledge.store`, `tarel.lineage.change_store`, `tarel.lineage.store`, `tarel.logical_joins.store`, `tarel.object_bindings.application`, `tarel.object_families.proposals`, `tarel.object_families.store`, `tarel.reference_mapping.store`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.store`, `tarel.semantics.store`, `tarel.sources.store`, `tarel.sync.store`, `tarel.topology.store`, `tarel.workspaces.store`

## `tarel.bundle`

### `src/tarel/bundle/application.py`

Use cases for offline bundles. The CLI dialog and the plain commands both call only these.

- **Input:** parameters `folder: Path`, `hub: Path | None`, `on_step: StepCallback`, `output: Path`, `path: Path`, `recipe: Recipe`, `replace: bool`, `test_install: bool`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `list_components_use_case`: list components (use case); returns `tuple[Component, ...]`. Calls `available_components`.
    - `plan_bundle_use_case`: Resolve the recipe into exact requirements without downloading anything. Calls `select_components`, `BundlePlan`, `available_components`, `pip_platforms`; raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `build_bundle_use_case`: Build the bundle for ``recipe`` into the ZIP file ``output``. Calls `build_bundle`; raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `default_output`: default output; returns `Path`.
    - `load_recipe_use_case`: load recipe (use case); returns `Recipe`. Raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `save_recipe_use_case`: Write the recipe next to the bundle; like the bundle, never through a planted link. Calls `share_descriptor`.
- **Output:** returns `BundlePlan`, `BundleReport`, `Path`, `Recipe`, `tuple[Component, ...]`; deletes files; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel`, `tarel.bundle.builder`, `tarel.bundle.catalog`, `tarel.bundle.contracts`, `tarel.bundle.targets`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli._bundle_dialog`, `tarel.cli.bundle`

### `src/tarel/bundle/builder.py`

Build one offline bundle: collect wheels, pin them by hash, prove they install, and zip them.

- **Input:** parameters `handle: int`, `on_step: StepCallback`, `output: Path`, `plan: BundlePlan`, `tarel_from: Path | None`, `test_install: bool`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `build_bundle`: build bundle; returns `BundleReport`. Calls `ensure_pip`, `BundleReport`, `complete_for_target`, `run_pip`, `target_arguments`; raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `share_descriptor`: Make a new file readable by others through its descriptor; a path could be swapped.
- **Output:** returns `BundleReport`; deletes files; writes files; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.bundle.contracts`, `tarel.bundle.pip`, `tarel.bundle.resolve`, `tarel.bundle.targets`
- **Used by:** `tarel.bundle.application`

### `src/tarel/bundle/catalog.py`

The parts a bundle can contain: core extras and the packages listed in a hub checkout.

- **Input:** parameters `available: tuple[Component, ...]`, `chosen: tuple[str, ...]`, `hub_root: Path | None`, `hub_root: Path`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `hub_components`: Read the installable packages of one hub checkout. Calls `Component`; raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `available_components`: available components; returns `tuple[Component, ...]`. Raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `select_components`: The chosen components plus the ones that are always included, in catalog order. Raises `BundleFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `tuple[Component, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.bundle.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.bundle.application`

### `src/tarel/bundle/contracts.py`

Records for offline bundles: the target machine, the recipe, the plan, and the build report.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `text: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `BundleFailure`: A bundle could not be planned, built, or read.
    - `Target`: The machine that will install the bundle. Only what wheel selection depends on.
    - `Target.slug`: Short, file-name-safe identifier, e.g. ``windows-x86_64-py3.12``.
    - `Target.label`: Human description, e.g. ``Windows · x86-64 · Python 3.12``.
    - `Target.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `Target.from_dict`: from dict; returns `Target`.
    - `Component`: One selectable part of a bundle: a core extra or a hub package.
    - `Component.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
    - `Recipe`: Everything needed to build the same bundle again.
    - `Recipe.to_toml`: to toml; returns `str`.
    - `Recipe.from_toml`: from toml; returns `Recipe`.
    - `BundlePlan`: What a build will download, before anything is downloaded.
    - `BundlePlan.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
    - `BundledPackage`: record with fields name, version, license.
    - `BundleReport`: The finished bundle and how far it was proven to install.
    - `BundleReport.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
- **Output:** returns `Recipe`, `Target`, `dict[str, Any]`, `dict[str, str]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.bundle.application`, `tarel.bundle.builder`, `tarel.bundle.catalog`, `tarel.bundle.pip`, `tarel.bundle.resolve`, `tarel.bundle.targets`, `tarel.cli._bundle_dialog`, `tarel.cli.bundle`

### `src/tarel/bundle/pip.py`

Run pip as a subprocess of the current interpreter, with failures turned into BundleFailure.

- **Input:** parameters `arguments: Sequence[str]`, `code: str`, `python: Path | None`, `target: Target`
- **Processing:**
    - `run_pip`: Run ``python -m pip`` and return stdout; raise with pip's last error lines on failure. Raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `ensure_pip`: ensure pip; returns `None`.
    - `target_arguments`: pip options that select wheels for ``target`` instead of for this machine. Calls `pip_platforms`.
- **Output:** returns `list[str]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.bundle.contracts`, `tarel.bundle.targets`
- **Used by:** `tarel.bundle.builder`

### `src/tarel/bundle/resolve.py`

Complete a wheel folder for the target's environment markers.

- **Input:** parameters `download: Callable[[list[str]], None]`, `local: Iterable[str]`, `patch: str`, `roots: Iterable[str]`, `target: Target`, `wheel: Path`, `wheels: Path`
- **Processing:**
    - `marker_environments`: marker environments; returns `tuple[dict[str, str], ...]`.
    - `marker_environment`: marker environment; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `complete_for_target`: Download until the closure of ``roots`` is complete, then delete unused wheels. Raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `wheel_metadata`: wheel metadata; returns `Any`. Raises `BundleFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `Any`, `dict[str, str]`, `tuple[dict[str, str], ...]`; deletes files
- **Uses:** `tarel.bundle.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.bundle.builder`

### `src/tarel/bundle/targets.py`

Describe a target machine and translate it into the wheel platform tags pip understands.

- **Input:** parameters `host: Target`, `line: str`, `target: Target`, `text: str`, `version: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `host_profile`: The profile line this machine would print; lets "same as this machine" share one parser.
    - `host_target`: host target; returns `Target`.
    - `parse_profile`: Read the line printed by :data:`PROFILE_COMMAND`. Calls `Target`; raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `macos_baseline`: The wheel-tag version of a macOS release: 10.x keeps its minor, 11 and later do not.
    - `parse_target`: Read ``host``, a profile line, or a slug such as ``linux-x86_64-py3.12-glibc2.28``. Calls `Target`; raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `pip_platforms`: Every platform tag a wheel may carry and still install on ``target``, best first. Raises `BundleFailure`.
    - `can_test_install`: True when a bundle for ``target`` can really be installed on ``host`` as a test.
- **Output:** returns `Target`, `bool`, `str`, `tuple[str, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.bundle.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.bundle.application`, `tarel.bundle.builder`, `tarel.bundle.pip`, `tarel.cli._bundle_dialog`, `tarel.cli.bundle`

### `src/tarel/cli/_bundle_dialog.py`

The interactive ``tarel bundle`` dialog: three short steps, sensible defaults, one summary.

- **Input:** parameters `default: int`, `default: str`, `hub: Path | None`, `items: Sequence[Component]`, `label: str`, `number: int`, `options: Sequence[tuple[str, str]]`, `output: Path`, `preset: set[str]`, `question: str`, `recipe: Recipe`, `recipe_path: Path` …; reads environment variables
- **Processing:**
    - `Cancelled`: The person left the dialog (Ctrl+C, Ctrl+D, or declined the summary).
    - `Dialog`: record with fields style, ask, say, folder.
    - `Dialog.rule`: rule; returns `None`.
    - `Dialog.heading`: heading; returns `None`.
    - `Dialog.prompt`: prompt; returns `str`.
    - `Dialog.choose`: One numbered choice. Returns the zero-based index.
    - `Dialog.choose_many`: choose many; returns `set[str]`.
    - `Dialog.done`: done; returns `None`.
    - `Dialog.run`: run; returns `BundleReport`. Calls `Recipe`, `default_output`, `save_recipe_use_case`.
    - `Dialog.ask_target`: ask target; returns `Target`. Calls `host_target`.
    - `Dialog.paste_profile`: paste profile; returns `Target`. Calls `parse_profile`.
    - `Dialog.pick_target`: pick target; returns `Target`. Calls `Target`.
    - `Dialog.ask_version`: ask version; returns `str`.
    - `Dialog.ask_hub`: ask hub; returns `Path | None`. Calls `list_components_use_case`.
    - `Dialog.ask_components`: ask components; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Calls `list_components_use_case`.
    - `Dialog.summary`: summary; returns `None`. Calls `list_components_use_case`.
    - `Dialog.build`: build; returns `BundleReport`. Calls `build_bundle_use_case`.
    - `Dialog.finish`: finish; returns `None`.
- **Output:** returns `BundleReport`, `Path | None`, `Target`, `int`, `set[str]`, `str`, `tuple[str, ...]`; prints to the terminal
- **Uses:** `tarel`, `tarel.bundle.application`, `tarel.bundle.contracts`, `tarel.bundle.targets`, `tarel.cli._terminal`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.bundle`

### `src/tarel/cli/_output.py`

Text and JSON renderers shared by several command groups.

- **Input:** parameters `ensure_ascii: bool`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `output_format: str`, `path: Path | None`, `payload: object`, `result: CatalogResult`, `result: ProbeResult`, `tasks: tuple[AnnotationTask, ...]`
- **Processing:**
    - `render_annotation_plan`: render annotation plan; returns `None`.
    - `render_catalog`: render catalog; returns `None`.
    - `render_graph_summary`: render graph summary; returns `None`.
    - `render_probe`: render probe; returns `None`.
    - `print_json`: Print ``payload`` as every ``--format json`` command does: indented, keys sorted.
- **Output:** prints JSON to stdout; prints to the terminal
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.contracts`, `tarel.connectors.contracts`, `tarel.graph.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.annotation`, `tarel.cli.binding`, `tarel.cli.bundle`, `tarel.cli.concept`, `tarel.cli.connector`, `tarel.cli.context`, `tarel.cli.context_expand`, `tarel.cli.demo`, `tarel.cli.discovery`, `tarel.cli.entity`, `tarel.cli.family`, `tarel.cli.family_proposals`, `tarel.cli.focus`, `tarel.cli.graph`, `tarel.cli.graph_reads`, `tarel.cli.hub`, `tarel.cli.index`, `tarel.cli.knowledge`, `tarel.cli.lineage`, `tarel.cli.logical_join`, `tarel.cli.model`, `tarel.cli.package`, `tarel.cli.provider`, `tarel.cli.reference_mapping`, `tarel.cli.relationship`, `tarel.cli.retrieval`, `tarel.cli.search`, `tarel.cli.semantic`, `tarel.cli.source`, `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.cli.topology`, `tarel.cli.workspace`

### `src/tarel/cli/_terminal.py`

Small, dependency-free terminal styling for interactive commands.

- **Input:** parameters `name: str`, `stream: TextIO`, `value: str`; reads environment variables; reads stdin
- **Processing:**
    - `Style`: record with fields color, unicode, live.
    - `Style.for_stream`: for stream; returns `Style`.
    - `Style.symbol`: symbol; returns `str`.
    - `Style.text`: Make arbitrary text printable, e.g. the ``·`` inside target labels.
    - `Style.bold`: bold; returns `str`.
    - `Style.dim`: dim; returns `str`.
    - `Style.accent`: accent; returns `str`.
    - `Style.good`: good; returns `str`.
    - `Style.bad`: bad; returns `str`.
    - `is_interactive`: is interactive; returns `bool`.
- **Output:** returns `Style`, `bool`, `str`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli._bundle_dialog`, `tarel.cli.bundle`

### `src/tarel/cli/annotation.py`

`tarel annotation`: plan, apply, review and edit semantic annotations.

- **Input:** parameters `args: argparse.Namespace`, `subcommands: Subcommands`; reads files; reads stdin
- **Processing:**
    - `register`: Add the `annotation` command group to the top-level parser. Calls `add_annotation_knowledge_arguments`, `add_format_argument`.
    - `dispatch`: Run a parsed `annotation` command; return ``None`` for other commands.
    - `_run_plan`: plan; returns `int`. Calls `render_annotation_plan`, `plan_focus_annotations_use_case`, `plan_annotations_use_case`.
    - `_run_next`: next; returns `int`. Calls `plan_focus_annotations_use_case`, `plan_annotations_use_case`, `print_json`.
    - `_run_apply`: apply; returns `int`. Calls `apply_annotation_use_case`.
    - `_run_list`: list; returns `int`. Calls `list_annotation_reviews_use_case`.
    - `_run_show`: show; returns `int`. Calls `show_annotation_use_case`.
    - `_run_edit`: edit; returns `int`. Calls `edit_annotation_use_case`.
    - `_run_decide`: decide; returns `int`. Calls `decide_annotation_use_case`.
- **Output:** returns `int | None`; prints JSON to stdout; prints to the terminal
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.annotations.contracts`, `tarel.annotations.review`, `tarel.cli._args`, `tarel.cli._output`

## `tarel.connectors`

### `src/tarel/connectors/application.py`

Connector use cases: check, probe, discover, sample and profile a configured source.

- **Input:** parameters `config_path: Path | None`, `database: str | None`, `include_values: bool`, `limit: int`, `name: str`, `namespace: str | None`, `namespace: str`, `object_name: str`, `output: Path | None`, `path: Path | None`, `row_limit: int`, `section: dict[str, Any]` …; loads stored documents; reads environment variables
- **Processing:**
    - `list_connectors_use_case`: list connectors (use case); returns `tuple[ConnectorCheck, ...]`. Calls `list_connectors`.
    - `check_connector_use_case`: check connector (use case); returns `ConnectorCheck`. Calls `check_connector`.
    - `ConfiguredConnector`: A loaded connector with its resolved target; URL and login never appear in repr.
    - `configured_connector`: configured connector; returns `ConfiguredConnector`. Calls `load_connector`, `resolve_credential`; raises `ConnectorFailure`.
    - `probe_connector_use_case`: probe connector (use case); returns `ProbeResult`. Calls `ProbeRequest`.
    - `discover_catalog_use_case`: discover catalog (use case); returns `CatalogResult`. Calls `CatalogRequest`.
    - `sample_connector_use_case`: sample connector (use case); returns `SampleResult`. Calls `SampleRequest`.
    - `profile_connector_use_case`: profile connector (use case); returns `ObjectProfileResult`. Calls `ObjectProfileRequest`; raises `ConnectorFailure`.
    - `scaffold_connector_use_case`: scaffold connector (use case); returns `ScaffoldResult`. Calls `scaffold_connector`.
    - `read_config`: read config; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Raises `ConnectorFailure`.
    - `connection_url`: connection url; returns `str`. Raises `ConnectorFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `CatalogResult`, `ConfiguredConnector`, `ConnectorCheck`, `ObjectProfileResult`, `ProbeResult`, `SampleResult`, `ScaffoldResult`, `dict[str, Any]`, `str`, `tuple[ConnectorCheck, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.authoring`, `tarel.connectors.contracts`, `tarel.connectors.credentials`, `tarel.connectors.host`
- **Used by:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.cli.connector`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.relationships.application`, `tarel.sdk.connectors`, `tarel.sdk.setup`, `tarel.sources.application`

### `src/tarel/connectors/authoring.py`

Create isolated, inactive connector candidates for humans and coding agents.

- **Input:** parameters `name: str`, `output: Path | None`
- **Processing:**
    - `ScaffoldResult`: record with fields name, path.
    - `scaffold_connector`: scaffold connector; returns `ScaffoldResult`. Raises `ConnectorFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `ScaffoldResult`; writes files
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.connectors.application`, `tarel.sdk.setup`

### `src/tarel/connectors/catalog.py`

Strict loading and validation for caller-observed catalogs.

- **Input:** parameters `catalog: CatalogResult`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `path: Path`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `load_catalog_result`: load catalog result; returns `CatalogResult`. Raises `ConnectorFailure`.
    - `catalog_result_from_dict`: catalog result from dict; returns `CatalogResult`. Calls `CatalogResult`; raises `ConnectorFailure`.
    - `validate_catalog_result`: validate catalog result; returns `None`. Raises `ConnectorFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `CatalogResult`
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.graph`, `tarel.graph.application`

### `src/tarel/connectors/contracts.py`

Small contracts shared by TAREL and source connectors.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `request: CatalogRequest`, `request: ObjectProfileRequest`, `request: ProbeRequest`, `request: RelationshipProbeRequest`, `request: SampleRequest`
- **Processing:**
    - `ConnectorFailure`: A connector failure safe to present at the CLI boundary.
    - `Credential`: A login the host resolved from configuration; the secret never appears in repr or output.
    - `ConnectorManifest`: record with fields contract_version, name, version, source_type, entrypoint, extra, capabilities, dependencies ….
    - `ConnectorManifest.from_mapping`: from mapping; returns `ConnectorManifest`.
    - `ConnectorCheck`: record with fields name, version, source_type, available, capabilities, permissions, missing_dependencies, extra ….
    - `ConnectorCheck.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ProbeRequest`: record with fields url, database, credential.
    - `ProbeResult`: record with fields connector, source_type, server_name, database_name, product_version, product_level, edition, capabilities ….
    - `ProbeResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `CatalogRequest`: record with fields url, database, namespace, credential.
    - `CatalogField`: record with fields name, position, data_type, nullable, description, is_primary_key.
    - `CatalogField.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `CatalogObject`: record with fields namespace, name, kind, fields, description, primary_key.
    - `CatalogObject.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `CatalogRelationship`: record with fields name, from_namespace, from_object, from_fields, to_namespace, to_object, to_fields, kind.
    - `CatalogRelationship.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `CatalogResult`: record with fields connector, source_type, catalog, dialect, objects, relationships.
    - `CatalogResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `SampleRequest`: record with fields url, database, namespace, object_name, limit, credential.
    - `SampleResult`: record with fields connector, catalog, namespace, object_name, selected_fields, omitted_fields, ordered_by, rows ….
    - `SampleResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ValueCount`: record with fields value, count.
    - `ValueCount.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ColumnProfile`: record with fields name, data_type, status, reason, non_null_count, null_count, distinct_count, min_value ….
    - `ColumnProfile.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ObjectProfileRequest`: record with fields url, database, namespace, object_name, row_limit, small_domain_limit, include_values, credential.
    - `ObjectProfileResult`: record with fields connector, catalog, namespace, object_name, row_limit, rows_profiled, complete, ordered_by ….
    - `ObjectProfileResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RelationshipPair`: record with fields from_namespace, from_object, from_field, to_namespace, to_object, to_field.
    - `RelationshipPair.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `RelationshipProbeRequest`: record with fields url, database, pairs, row_limit, credential.
    - `RelationshipPairProfile`: record with fields pair, source_non_null_count, source_distinct_count, target_non_null_count, target_distinct_count, overlap_count, profile_row_limit.
    - `RelationshipPairProfile.source_coverage`: source coverage; returns `float`.
    - `RelationshipPairProfile.target_coverage`: target coverage; returns `float`.
    - `RelationshipPairProfile.target_uniqueness`: target uniqueness; returns `float`.
    - `RelationshipPairProfile.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RelationshipProbeResult`: record with fields connector, catalog, profiles.
    - `RelationshipProbeResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `Connector`: record with fields manifest.
    - `Connector.probe`: probe; returns `ProbeResult`.
    - `Connector.discover_catalog`: discover catalog; returns `CatalogResult`.
    - `Connector.sample_rows`: sample rows; returns `SampleResult`.
    - `ObjectProfileConnector`: class based on Protocol.
    - `ObjectProfileConnector.profile_object`: profile object; returns `ObjectProfileResult`.
    - `RelationshipProbeConnector`: class based on Protocol.
    - `RelationshipProbeConnector.probe_relationships`: probe relationships; returns `RelationshipProbeResult`.
- **Output:** returns `CatalogResult`, `ConnectorManifest`, `ObjectProfileResult`, `ProbeResult`, `RelationshipProbeResult`, `SampleResult`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `float`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.annotations.tasks`, `tarel.cli._output`, `tarel.cli.connector`, `tarel.cli.relationship`, `tarel.connectors.application`, `tarel.connectors.authoring`, `tarel.connectors.catalog`, `tarel.connectors.credentials`, `tarel.connectors.host`, `tarel.connectors.sqlite.connector`, `tarel.connectors.sqlserver.connector`, `tarel.enrichment`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.graph.build`, `tarel.relationships.application`, `tarel.relationships.core`, `tarel.sdk.annotation`, `tarel.sdk.connectors`, `tarel.sdk.graph`, `tarel.sdk.relationship`, `tarel.sdk.sources`, `tarel.sources.application`

### `src/tarel/context/application.py`

Context use cases: compile, prefix, describe, compare and assess context packets.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str] | None`, `areas: tuple[str, ...]`, `bm25_weight: float | None`, `current: ContextResult | ContextPacketSnapshot | dict[str, object] | Path`, `focuses: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph_name: str`, `graphs: tuple[str, ...]`, `left: Path`, `logical_hints: str | None`, `max_characters: int`, `max_fields_per_object: int`, `max_hops: int` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `compile_context_use_case`: compile context (use case); returns `ContextResult`. Calls `validate_bm25_weight`, `resolve_graph_object_scope`, `selected_annotation_states`, `add_logical_context_hints_use_case`, `project_graph_scope`, `ContextScope` …; uses the graph store.
    - `compile_workspace_context_use_case`: compile workspace context (use case); returns `ContextResult`. Calls `validate_bm25_weight`, `load_workspace_scope`, `selected_annotation_states`, `project_workspace_scope`, `ContextScope`, `add_logical_context_hints_use_case` ….
    - `compile_context_prefix_use_case`: compile context prefix (use case); returns `ContextResult`. Calls `resolve_graph_object_scope`, `selected_annotation_states`, `compile_context_prefix`, `add_logical_context_hints_use_case`, `project_graph_scope`, `ContextScope` …; uses the graph store.
    - `compile_workspace_context_prefix_use_case`: compile workspace context prefix (use case); returns `ContextResult`. Calls `load_workspace_scope`, `selected_annotation_states`, `project_workspace_scope`, `compile_context_prefix`, `add_logical_context_hints_use_case`, `ContextScope`.
    - `diff_context_packets_use_case`: diff context packets (use case); returns `ContextPacketDiff`. Calls `diff_context_packets`, `load_context_packet`.
    - `context_packet_diff_views_use_case`: Load two packet files once and return their technical and guidance views. Calls `load_context_packet`, `diff_context_packets`, `context_delta`.
    - `describe_context_use_case`: describe context (use case); returns `ContextBrief`. Calls `context_brief`.
    - `compare_context_use_case`: compare context (use case); returns `ContextDelta`. Calls `context_delta`, `load_context_packet`.
    - `context_packet_brief_use_case`: context packet brief (use case); returns `ContextBrief`. Calls `load_context_packet`.
    - `compare_context_packets_use_case`: compare context packets (use case); returns `ContextDelta`. Calls `load_context_packet`.
    - `context_packet_impact_use_case`: context packet impact (use case); returns `ContextPacketImpact`. Calls `graph_revision`, `load_context_packet`, `context_packet_graph_identity`, `context_packet_impact`; uses the graph change store, graph store.
- **Output:** returns `ContextBrief`, `ContextDelta`, `ContextPacketDiff`, `ContextPacketImpact`, `ContextResult`, `tuple[ContextPacketDiff, ContextDelta]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.context.guidance`, `tarel.context.hints_application`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.context.packets`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.retrieval.ranking`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.search`, `tarel.workspaces.application`, `tarel.workspaces.projection`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.context`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.sdk.context`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`

### `src/tarel/context/caching.py`

Harness-neutral prompt blocks derived from a deterministic context packet.

- **Input:** parameters `packet: ContextResult`
- **Processing:**
    - `ContextCacheParts`: Stable and request-specific blocks for a consumer-managed prompt cache.
    - `ContextCacheParts.cache_key`: cache key; returns `str`.
    - `ContextCacheParts.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `split_context_packet`: Split a packet without adding provider-specific cache instructions. Calls `canonical_json`.
- **Output:** returns `ContextCacheParts`, `dict[str, str]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.output`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.context`

### `src/tarel/context/compiler.py`

Compile bounded agent context from lexical search and reviewed graph relationships.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str]`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `hints: LogicalContextHints`, `max_characters: int`, `max_fields_per_object: int`, `max_hops: int`, `max_joins: int`, `max_objects: int`, `namespace: str | None`, `object_ids: tuple[str, ...]`, `query: str`, `result: ContextResult` …
- **Processing:**
    - `ContextFailure`: Raised when a context operation is rejected.
    - `compile_context`: Build context without querying source data or invoking an LLM. Calls `search_graph`.
    - `compile_context_from_search`: Expand caller-supplied retrieval anchors through reviewed graph relationships. Calls `ContextResult`, `graph_revision`, `usable_relationships`, `ContextPath`, `ContextScope`.
    - `compile_context_from_objects`: Compile context from exact physical objects without another fuzzy search. Calls `SearchResults`, `SearchHit`.
    - `compile_context_prefix`: Compile one query-independent graph or workspace scope for prompt-prefix reuse. Calls `ContextResult`, `graph_revision`, `ContextScope`, `usable_relationships`.
    - `with_logical_context_hints`: Refit the existing total budget after attaching optional, non-traversable hints.
- **Output:** returns `ContextResult`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.context.hints`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.errors`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.relationships.core`, `tarel.search`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.context`, `tarel.cli.grounding`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.grounding`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.context.guidance`, `tarel.context.hints_application`, `tarel.context.packets`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.sdk.context`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`, `tarel.ui.server`

### `src/tarel/context/grounding.py`

Agent-facing grounding contract over context and reviewed lineage.

- **Input:** none (constants or declarations only)
- **Processing:**
    - `SourceTarget`: A non-secret execution target for objects selected into context.
    - `SourceTarget.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageTarget`: One explicitly selected lineage document and its immutable revision.
    - `LineageTarget.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `GroundingAsset`: One exactly resolved object or field and its non-secret source target.
    - `GroundingAsset.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `GroundingBundle`: Deterministic inputs for a BI agent turn.
    - `GroundingBundle.stable_dict`: stable dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `GroundingBundle.dynamic_dict`: dynamic dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `GroundingBundle.identity_dict`: identity dict; returns `dict[str, str]`. Calls `canonical_hash`.
    - `GroundingBundle.stable_hash`: stable hash; returns `str`. Calls `canonical_hash`.
    - `GroundingBundle.dynamic_hash`: dynamic hash; returns `str`. Calls `canonical_hash`.
    - `GroundingBundle.bundle_hash`: bundle hash; returns `str`.
    - `GroundingBundle.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `GroundingBundle.canonical_json`: canonical json; returns `str`.
    - `GroundingBundle.stable_prompt`: Render a query-independent, cache-friendly semantic prefix. Calls `canonical_json`.
    - `GroundingBundle.dynamic_prompt`: Render current-turn retrieval, evidence, omissions, and trace. Calls `canonical_json`.
- **Output:** returns `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.traversal`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.grounding`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.grounding`

### `src/tarel/context/grounding_application.py`

Application use cases for agent-facing semantic grounding.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str] | None`, `areas: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph_name: str`, `graphs: tuple[str, ...]`, `lineage_limit: int`, `lineage_mode: str`, `lineage_names: tuple[str, ...]`, `lineage_states: frozenset[str] | None`, `logical_hints: str | None`, `max_characters: int`, `max_fields_per_object: int`, `max_hops: int` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `describe_grounding_asset_use_case`: Resolve an asset exactly; fuzzy discovery remains a separate operation. Calls `load_graph_use_case`, `show_annotation_use_case`, `GroundingAsset`, `SourceTarget`, `graph_revision`.
    - `compile_graph_grounding_use_case`: compile graph grounding (use case); returns `GroundingBundle`. Calls `compile_context_use_case`, `load_graph_use_case`.
    - `compile_workspace_grounding_use_case`: compile workspace grounding (use case); returns `GroundingBundle`. Calls `resolve_workspace_scope_use_case`, `compile_workspace_context_use_case`, `load_graph_use_case`.
- **Output:** returns `GroundingAsset`, `GroundingBundle`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.context.grounding`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.revision`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sources.contracts`, `tarel.sources.store`, `tarel.workspaces.application`, `tarel.workspaces.projection`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.grounding`, `tarel.sdk.grounding`

### `src/tarel/context/guidance.py`

Compact, deterministic orientation over versioned context packets.

- **Input:** parameters `current: ContextResult | ContextPacketSnapshot | dict[str, object]`, `packet: ContextResult | ContextPacketSnapshot | dict[str, object]`, `previous: ContextResult | ContextPacketSnapshot | dict[str, object]`
- **Processing:**
    - `ContextGap`: One evidence-backed limitation of the compiled context.
    - `ContextGap.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextContinuity`: Small state a harness can retain beside its own conversation notes.
    - `ContextContinuity.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextBrief`: A small agent- and human-readable projection of one context packet.
    - `ContextBrief.continuity`: continuity; returns `ContextContinuity`.
    - `ContextBrief.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextBrief.text`: text; returns `str`.
    - `ContextGapChange`: record with fields code, category, before, after, evidence_changed.
    - `ContextGapChange.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextDelta`: Product-facing change summary between two validated packets.
    - `ContextDelta.identical`: identical; returns `bool`.
    - `ContextDelta.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextDelta.text`: text; returns `str`.
    - `context_brief`: Project a validated context packet into a bounded orientation summary.
    - `context_delta`: Describe what changed for a continuing analysis conversation.
- **Output:** returns `ContextBrief`, `ContextContinuity`, `ContextDelta`, `bool`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.context.packets`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.context`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.context`

Value-free logical hints projected into an optional context section.

### `src/tarel/context/packets.py`

Load, validate, and compare serialized TAREL context packets.

- **Input:** parameters `graph: GraphDocument`, `left: ContextPacketSnapshot`, `packet: ContextPacketSnapshot`, `path: Path`, `payload: dict[str, object]`, `report: GraphRefreshReport | None`, `right: ContextPacketSnapshot`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `ContextPacketSnapshot`: record with fields contract_version, stable, dynamic, stable_hash, dynamic_hash, packet_hash.
    - `ContextPacketDiff`: record with fields left_contract, right_contract, left_packet_hash, right_packet_hash, stable_changed, dynamic_changed, graph_revision_changed, scope_changed ….
    - `ContextPacketDiff.identical`: identical; returns `bool`.
    - `ContextPacketDiff.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextPacketImpact`: record with fields status, graph, packet_revision, current_revision, exact, matched_changes, reason.
    - `ContextPacketImpact.affected`: affected; returns `bool | None`.
    - `ContextPacketImpact.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `load_context_packet`: load context packet; returns `ContextPacketSnapshot`. Raises `ContextFailure`.
    - `context_packet_from_dict`: context packet from dict; returns `ContextPacketSnapshot`. Calls `canonical_hash`; raises `ContextFailure`.
    - `diff_context_packets`: diff context packets; returns `ContextPacketDiff`.
    - `context_packet_impact`: context packet impact; returns `ContextPacketImpact`. Calls `graph_revision`; raises `ContextFailure`.
    - `context_packet_graph_identity`: context packet graph identity; returns `tuple[str, str]`.
- **Output:** returns `ContextPacketDiff`, `ContextPacketImpact`, `ContextPacketSnapshot`, `bool`, `bool | None`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[str, str]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.graph.changes`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.refresh`, `tarel.graph.revision`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.context_expand`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.guidance`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.sdk.context`

### `src/tarel/demo.py`

Deterministic local demo sources for exercising public TAREL workflows.

## `tarel.discovery`

### `src/tarel/discovery/_parse.py`

Strict readers for JSON discovery documents; each raises ``DiscoveryFailure`` on bad input.

- **Input:** parameters `choices: frozenset[str]`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `label: str`, `limit: int`, `maximum: int`, `minimum: int`, `optional: set[str] | None`, `pattern: re.Pattern[str]`, `required: set[str]`, `value: object`
- **Processing:**
    - `require_fields`: require fields; returns `None`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `require_object`: require object; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `require_text`: require text; returns `str`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `optional_text`: optional text; returns `str | None`.
    - `require_identifier`: require identifier; returns `str`.
    - `optional_identifier`: optional identifier; returns `str | None`.
    - `require_pattern`: require pattern; returns `str`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `require_sha256`: require sha256; returns `str`.
    - `require_choice`: require choice; returns `str`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `string_array`: string array; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `identifier_array`: identifier array; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `integer_array`: integer array; returns `tuple[int, ...]`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `require_integer`: require integer; returns `int`.
    - `optional_integer`: optional integer; returns `int | None`.
    - `bounded_integer`: bounded integer; returns `int`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `require_rate`: require rate; returns `float`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `optional_rate`: optional rate; returns `float | None`.
    - `require_boolean`: require boolean; returns `bool`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `bool`, `dict[str, Any]`, `float`, `float | None`, `int`, `int | None`, `str`, `str | None`, `tuple[int, ...]`, `tuple[str, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.discovery.actions`, `tarel.discovery.programs`, `tarel.discovery.runs`

### `src/tarel/discovery/actions.py`

The discovery state machine: which actions a run allows next and how each changes it.

- **Input:** parameters `action: str`, `actor: str`, `payload: dict[str, Any]`, `run: DiscoveryRun`
- **Processing:**
    - `allowed_discovery_actions`: allowed discovery actions; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Calls `latest_identity_reflection`.
    - `apply_discovery_action`: apply discovery action; returns `DiscoveryRun`. Calls `require_choice`, `require_fields`, `require_text`; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `DiscoveryRun`, `tuple[str, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery._parse`, `tarel.discovery.contracts`, `tarel.discovery.identity`, `tarel.discovery.programs`, `tarel.discovery.runs`
- **Used by:** `tarel.discovery.advice`, `tarel.discovery.application`, `tarel.discovery.tasks`

### `src/tarel/discovery/advice.py`

Provider advice for a discovery run: a bounded structured review of its candidates.

- **Input:** parameters `count: int`, `expected_revision: str`, `model: str | None`, `run_id: str`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`, `timeout: float`; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `DiscoveryAdviceResult`: record with fields run, path, provider, proposed_count.
    - `advise_discovery_run_use_case`: advise discovery run (use case); returns `DiscoveryAdviceResult`. Calls `validate_current_graph`, `load_provider`, `allowed_discovery_actions`, `StructuredRequest`, `validate_program_bindings`, `apply_discovery_action`; uses the discovery store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`, `ProviderFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `DiscoveryAdviceResult`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.actions`, `tarel.discovery.checks`, `tarel.discovery.contracts`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.discovery.tasks`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.providers.contracts`, `tarel.providers.host`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.discovery`, `tarel.sdk.discovery`

### `src/tarel/discovery/application.py`

Discovery run use cases: start, load, list, submit steps and find candidates.

- **Input:** parameters `action: str`, `actor: str`, `advisor_provider: str | None`, `candidate_budget: int`, `expected_revision: str`, `graph_name: str | None`, `graph_name: str`, `identity_inspection: bool`, `include_exploratory: bool`, `kind: str | None`, `kind: str`, `limit: int` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `DiscoveryChangeResult`: record with fields run, path.
    - `DiscoveryMatch`: record with fields run_id, run_revision, graph_name, candidate, question, score.
    - `DiscoveryMatch.usage`: usage; returns `str`.
    - `DiscoveryMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `start_discovery_run_use_case`: start discovery run (use case); returns `DiscoveryChangeResult`. Calls `validate_sources`, `graph_revision`, `IdentityInspection`; uses the discovery store, graph store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `load_discovery_run_use_case`: load discovery run (use case); returns `DiscoveryRun`. Uses the discovery store.
    - `list_discovery_runs_use_case`: list discovery runs (use case); returns `tuple[DiscoveryRun, ...]`. Uses the discovery store.
    - `submit_discovery_step_use_case`: submit discovery step (use case); returns `DiscoveryChangeResult`. Calls `validate_current_graph`, `apply_discovery_action`, `validate_observation_policy`, `validate_identity_inventory_policy`, `validate_identity_inventory_bindings`, `validate_entity_alias_policy` …; uses the discovery store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `find_discovery_candidates_use_case`: find discovery candidates (use case); returns `tuple[DiscoveryMatch, ...]`. Calls `rank_bm25`, `graph_revision`, `validate_logical_program`; uses the graph store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `DiscoveryChangeResult`, `DiscoveryRun`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`, `tuple[DiscoveryMatch, ...]`, `tuple[DiscoveryRun, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.actions`, `tarel.discovery.checks`, `tarel.discovery.contracts`, `tarel.discovery.coverage`, `tarel.discovery.identity`, `tarel.discovery.logical_program`, `tarel.discovery.programs`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.retrieval.bm25`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.discovery`, `tarel.sdk.discovery`

### `src/tarel/discovery/checks.py`

Checks that bind a discovery run to the current graph, sources and run policies.

- **Input:** parameters `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_name: str`, `payload: dict[str, Any]`, `program: LogicalJoinProgram`, `program: dict[str, Any]`, `run: DiscoveryRun`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`, `source_names: tuple[str, ...]`; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `validate_program_bindings`: validate program bindings; returns `None`. Calls `discovery_program_from_dict`, `resolve_field`; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_current_graph`: validate current graph; returns `GraphDocument`. Calls `graph_revision`; uses the graph store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_logical_program`: validate logical program; returns `None`. Calls `resolve_logical_endpoint_for_graph_use_case`.
    - `validate_sources`: validate sources; returns `None`. Uses the source store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_observation_policy`: validate observation policy; returns `None`. Uses the source store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_identity_inventory_policy`: validate identity inventory policy; returns `None`. Uses the source store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_entity_alias_policy`: validate entity alias policy; returns `None`. Uses the source store; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_identity_inventory_bindings`: validate identity inventory bindings; returns `None`. Calls `resolve_field`; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphDocument`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.contracts`, `tarel.discovery.identity`, `tarel.discovery.logical_program`, `tarel.discovery.programs`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.relationships.core`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`
- **Used by:** `tarel.discovery.advice`, `tarel.discovery.application`, `tarel.discovery.coverage_application`, `tarel.discovery.promotion`, `tarel.discovery.tasks`

### `src/tarel/discovery/programs.py`

Discovery programs: the declarative matching recipes a harness asks TAREL to evaluate.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `label: str`, `program: DiscoveryProgram`, `value: object`
- **Processing:**
    - `DiscoveryTransform`: record with fields kind, start, length.
    - `DiscoveryTransform.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `DiscoveryTransform.from_dict`: from dict; returns `DiscoveryTransform`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `optional_integer`; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `DiscoverySelfMatch`: Record identity semantics for comparisons within one graph object.
    - `DiscoverySelfMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `DiscoverySelfMatch.from_dict`: from dict; returns `DiscoverySelfMatch`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_text`, `require_choice`.
    - `DiscoveryProgram`: record with fields kind, source_fields, target_fields, source_transforms, target_transforms, comparison, blocking_field_indexes, contradiction_field_indexes ….
    - `DiscoveryProgram.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `DiscoveryProgram.from_dict`: from dict; returns `DiscoveryProgram`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `string_array`, `integer_array`, `optional_rate`; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `ReferenceMappingProgram`: A directed physical-field mapping hypothesis.
    - `ReferenceMappingProgram.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ReferenceMappingProgram.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ReferenceMappingProgram`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_text`, `require_choice`; raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `ReferenceMappingManifest`: Value-free identity and size of a caller-owned mapping artifact.
    - `ReferenceMappingManifest.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ReferenceMappingManifest.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ReferenceMappingManifest`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_sha256`, `bounded_integer`.
    - `discovery_program_from_dict`: discovery program from dict; returns `DiscoveryProgramType`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `validate_discovery_program`: validate discovery program; returns `None`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
    - `transform_matrix`: transform matrix; returns `tuple[tuple[DiscoveryTransform, ...], ...]`. Raises `DiscoveryFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `DiscoveryProgram`, `DiscoveryProgramType`, `DiscoverySelfMatch`, `DiscoveryTransform`, `ReferenceMappingManifest`, `ReferenceMappingProgram`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `tuple[tuple[DiscoveryTransform, ...], ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery._parse`, `tarel.discovery.contracts`, `tarel.discovery.logical_program`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.discovery`, `tarel.discovery.actions`, `tarel.discovery.application`, `tarel.discovery.checks`, `tarel.discovery.promotion`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.entity_resolution.candidates`, `tarel.entity_resolution.discovery`, `tarel.reference_mapping.application`, `tarel.reference_mapping.contracts`, `tarel.sdk`

### `src/tarel/entity_resolution/_parse.py`

Strict readers for entity resolution JSON; each raises ``EntityResolutionFailure``.

    - `require_object`: require object; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `require_text`: require text; returns `str`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `require_identifier`: require identifier; returns `str`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `require_sha256`: require sha256; returns `str`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `require_choice`: require choice; returns `str`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `string_array`: string array; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `identifier_array`: identifier array; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `require_integer`: require integer; returns `int`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `require_rate`: require rate; returns `float`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.

### `src/tarel/entity_resolution/application.py`

Application use cases for auditable entity-resolution candidates.

- **Input:** parameters `candidate: EntityResolutionCandidate`, `candidate_id: str`, `decision: str`, `expected_revision: str | None`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_name: str | None`, `graph_name: str`, `mode: str`, `object_reference: str`, `reason: str`, `record_key: str`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `EntityResolutionChangeResult`: record with fields candidate, path, changed.
    - `import_entity_resolution_candidate_use_case`: import entity resolution candidate (use case); returns `EntityResolutionChangeResult`. Calls `validate_entity_resolution_candidate`; uses the entity resolution store, graph store; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `load_entity_resolution_candidate_use_case`: load entity resolution candidate (use case); returns `EntityResolutionCandidate`. Uses the entity resolution store.
    - `list_entity_resolution_candidates_use_case`: list entity resolution candidates (use case); returns `tuple[EntityResolutionCandidate, ...]`. Uses the entity resolution store.
    - `find_entity_resolution_candidates_use_case`: find entity resolution candidates (use case); returns `tuple[EntityResolutionMatch, ...]`. Uses the graph store.
    - `find_entity_resolution_candidates_for_graph_use_case`: find entity resolution candidates for graph (use case); returns `tuple[EntityResolutionMatch, ...]`. Calls `graph_revision`; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `resolve_entity_aliases_use_case`: resolve entity aliases (use case); returns `tuple[EntityAliasMatch, ...]`. Calls `EntityAliasMatch`, `graph_revision`; uses the graph store; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `decide_entity_resolution_candidate_use_case`: decide entity resolution candidate (use case); returns `EntityResolutionChangeResult`. Calls `review_candidate`; uses the entity resolution store, graph store; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `EntityResolutionCandidate`, `EntityResolutionChangeResult`, `tuple[EntityAliasMatch, ...]`, `tuple[EntityResolutionCandidate, ...]`, `tuple[EntityResolutionMatch, ...]`; saves documents to a store

### `src/tarel/entity_resolution/candidates.py`

Entity resolution candidates, their validation and review, and resolved matches.

- **Input:** parameters `candidate: EntityResolutionCandidate`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `decision: str`, `include_identity_values: bool`, `include_revision: bool`, `reason: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `EntityResolutionCandidate`: record with fields id, graph_name, graph_revision, source_field_id, target_field_id, rule, evidence, provenance ….
    - `EntityResolutionCandidate.revision`: revision; returns `str`.
    - `EntityResolutionCandidate.human_reviewed`: human reviewed; returns `bool`.
    - `EntityResolutionCandidate.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `EntityResolutionCandidate.from_dict`: from dict; returns `EntityResolutionCandidate`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_object`, `require_identifier`, `require_text`, `require_sha256`, `require_choice`; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `EntityResolutionMatch`: record with fields candidate, source_reference, target_reference, query_linked_coverage.
    - `EntityResolutionMatch.usage`: usage; returns `str`.
    - `EntityResolutionMatch.requires_runtime_validation`: requires runtime validation; returns `bool`.
    - `EntityResolutionMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `EntityAliasMatch`: record with fields candidate_id, group, state, object_reference, record_key_field.
    - `EntityAliasMatch.usage`: usage; returns `str`.
    - `EntityAliasMatch.requires_runtime_validation`: requires runtime validation; returns `bool`.
    - `EntityAliasMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `validate_entity_resolution_candidate`: Raise ``EntityResolutionFailure`` unless identity, program and review are consistent.
    - `review_candidate`: review candidate; returns `EntityResolutionCandidate`. Raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `EntityResolutionCandidate`, `bool`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.coverage`, `tarel.discovery.identity`, `tarel.discovery.programs`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.entity_resolution._parse`, `tarel.entity_resolution.contracts`, `tarel.entity_resolution.evidence`

What supports an entity match: rules, evidence, provenance, quality and review.

    - `EntityResolutionRule`: record with fields kind, operations.
    - `EntityResolutionRule.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `EntityResolutionRule.from_dict`: from dict; returns `EntityResolutionRule`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `string_array`; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `EntityResolutionEvidence`: record with fields level, evaluated_count, matched_count, collision_count, counterexample_count, coverage, collision_rate, confidence.
    - `EntityResolutionEvidence.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `EntityResolutionEvidence.from_dict`: from dict; returns `EntityResolutionEvidence`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `require_integer`, `require_rate`.
    - `EntityResolutionProvenance`: record with fields run_id, producer, discovery_candidate_id, discovery_run_revision, observation_ids, promotion_reason, supersedes_candidate_id, source_names.
    - `EntityResolutionProvenance.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `EntityResolutionProvenance.from_dict`: from dict; returns `EntityResolutionProvenance`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_identifier`, `identifier_array`, `require_sha256`, `require_text`.
    - `EntityResolutionQuality`: record with fields score, rating, support_observation_id, challenge_observation_id, failed_observation_count, warnings, version.
    - `EntityResolutionQuality.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `EntityResolutionQuality.from_dict`: from dict; returns `EntityResolutionQuality`. Calls `require_fields`, `string_array`, `require_rate`, `require_choice`, `require_identifier`, `require_integer`; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `SelfEntityMatch`: Graph-bound identity semantics for distinct records of one object.
    - `SelfEntityMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `SelfEntityMatch.from_dict`: from dict; returns `SelfEntityMatch`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_text`, `string_array`, `require_choice`; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `EntityResolutionReview`: record with fields decision, reason, source.
    - `EntityResolutionReview.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `EntityResolutionReview.from_dict`: from dict; returns `EntityResolutionReview`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_text`, `require_choice`; raises `EntityResolutionFailure`.
    - `entity_resolution_quality_rating`: entity resolution quality rating; returns `str`.
- **Output:** returns `EntityResolutionEvidence`, `EntityResolutionProvenance`, `EntityResolutionQuality`, `EntityResolutionReview`, `EntityResolutionRule`, `SelfEntityMatch`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.entity_resolution._parse`, `tarel.entity_resolution.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.entity_resolution.candidates`, `tarel.entity_resolution.discovery`, `tarel.sdk`

### `src/tarel/expansion/contracts.py`

Bounded metadata deltas for an existing context packet, not a second query protocol.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `message: str`, `value: object`
- **Processing:**
    - `ContextExpansionFailure`: Raised when a context expansion operation is rejected.
    - `ExpansionTarget`: record with fields kind, graph, id, revision, limit, offset, handle.
    - `ExpansionTarget.reference`: reference; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ExpansionTarget.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ExpansionTarget`.
    - `ExpansionInput`: Caller-owned ephemeral selection; only its supplied manifest hash may leave this boundary.
    - `ExpansionInput.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ExpansionInput`.
    - `ExpansionItem`: record with fields target, usage, metadata, input_manifest_hash.
    - `ExpansionItem.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ContextExpansion`: record with fields base_packet_hash, items, omissions, max_characters, base_validation.
    - `ContextExpansion.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`. Calls `canonical_hash`.
    - `sha256`: sha256; returns `None`.
    - `invalid`: invalid; returns `None`.
- **Output:** returns `ExpansionInput`, `ExpansionTarget`, `dict[str, object]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.context_expand`, `tarel.expansion`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.context`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`, `tarel.ui.server`

- **Output:** returns `ExpansionItem`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.expansion.contracts`, `tarel.logical_joins.application`, `tarel.object_bindings.application`, `tarel.object_families.application`, `tarel.reference_mapping.application`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.application`, `tarel.topology.application`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`

Small process locks for cooperating local file writers.

- **Processing:**

### `src/tarel/graph/refresh.py`

Classify source changes and reconcile them with reviewed local knowledge.

- **Input:** parameters `current: GraphDocument`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `discovered: GraphDocument`, `graph: GraphDocument`

    - `StaleClaim`: record with fields claim_type, target_id, reference, previous_state, present, reasons, claim.
    - `StaleClaim.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `StaleClaim.from_dict`: from dict; returns `StaleClaim`. Raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `GraphRefreshReport`: record with fields before_revision, after_revision, changes, stale_claims, carried_annotations, carried_relationships, removed_annotated_nodes, removed_relationships ….
    - `GraphRefreshReport.affected_node_ids`: affected node ids; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `GraphRefreshReport.affected_edge_ids`: affected edge ids; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.

    - `GraphRefreshReport.from_dict`: from dict; returns `GraphRefreshReport`. Raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `refresh_graph`: Adopt ``discovered`` structure and carry reviewed knowledge over where it still applies. Calls `graph_revision`, `classify_graph_changes`; raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `unchanged_refresh_report`: Describe a fingerprint-identical observation without rebuilding the graph. Calls `graph_revision`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphRefreshReport`, `StaleClaim`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[GraphDocument, GraphRefreshReport]`, `tuple[str, ...]`

Experimental, rebuildable selective reads of authoritative JSON graphs.

- **Input:** parameters `expected_revision: str | None`, `include_fields: bool`, `limit: int`, `name: str`, `namespace: str | None`, `object_ids: tuple[str, ...] | None`, `object_ids: tuple[str, ...]`, `offset: int`, `store: FileGraphStore`; loads stored documents; opens database connections; reads files

    - `read_header`: read header; returns `GraphHeader`.
    - `read_slice`: read slice; returns `GraphSlice`. Calls `GraphDocument`; raises `GraphFailure`.

    - `rebuild_index`: rebuild index; returns `GraphHeader`.

### `src/tarel/graph/store.py`

Atomic local JSON graph store.

- **Input:** parameters `change: Callable[[GraphDocument], GraphDocument]`, `code: str`, `durable: bool`, `expected_revision: str | None`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `include_fields: bool`, `limit: int`, `message: str`, `name: str`, `namespace: str | None`, `object_ids: tuple[str, ...] | None`, `object_ids: tuple[str, ...]` …; loads stored documents; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `GraphStore`: Whole-document persistence boundary for local or shared graph stores.
    - `GraphStore.save`: save; returns `Path | str | None`.
    - `GraphStore.load`: load; returns `GraphDocument`.
    - `GraphStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileGraphStore`: class.
    - `FileGraphStore.save`: save; returns `Path`. Calls `graph_revision`; raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `FileGraphStore.create`: create; returns `Path`. Raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `FileGraphStore.update`: Apply a short local transformation to the latest graph under its lock. Raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `FileGraphStore.load`: load; returns `GraphDocument`. Raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `FileGraphStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileGraphStore.observation`: The record of the last read from the source, if this state made one. Raises `GraphFailure`.
    - `FileGraphStore.record_observation`: record observation; returns `None`. Calls `GraphObservation`, `utc_now`.
    - `FileGraphStore.record_failed_observation`: Keep the last good read: a failure proves nothing about what the source holds now. Calls `GraphObservation`, `utc_now`.
    - `FileGraphStore.header`: Read graph identity without materializing nodes after a visible cold bootstrap.
    - `FileGraphStore.read_slice`: read slice; returns `GraphSlice`.
    - `FileGraphStore.list_objects`: list objects; returns `GraphObjectPage`.
    - `FileGraphStore.rebuild_index`: Explicitly replace a damaged or missing optional selective-read cache.
    - `FileGraphStore.object_schema_hashes`: object schema hashes; returns `GraphObjectSchemaHashes`.
    - `FileGraphStore.path`: path; returns `Path`. Raises `GraphFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphDocument`, `GraphHeader`, `GraphObjectPage`, `GraphObjectSchemaHashes`, `GraphObservation | None`, `GraphSlice`, `Path`, `Path | str | None`, `tuple[GraphDocument, Path]`, `tuple[str, ...]`; replaces state files atomically
- **Uses:** `tarel.atomic`, `tarel.file_lock`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.observation`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.graph.selective`
- **Used by:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.graph.reads`, `tarel.graph.selective`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sources.application`, `tarel.topology.application`

## `tarel.hub`

### `src/tarel/hub/application.py`

Application use cases for TAREL Hub discovery, suggestions, and installation.

- **Input:** parameters `dry_run: bool`, `extra: str | None`, `hub_url: str | None`, `identifier: str`, `include_planned: bool`, `installed: bool | None`, `kind: str | None`, `offline: bool`, `path: Path | None`, `query: str`, `yes: bool`
- **Processing:**
    - `list_hub_extensions_use_case`: List TAREL Hub extensions with optional filters. Calls `load_hub_catalog`.
    - `show_hub_extension_use_case`: Inspect one TAREL Hub extension by id, package name, or entry-point name. Calls `load_hub_catalog`, `resolve_hub_extension`.
    - `search_hub_extensions_use_case`: Search TAREL Hub extensions by id, kind, capability, package, entry point, or evidence.
    - `suggest_hub_extensions_use_case`: Suggest TAREL Hub extensions for a local project directory without executing code. Calls `load_hub_catalog`, `suggest_hub_extensions`.
    - `install_hub_extension_use_case`: Plan or execute installation of one verified TAREL Hub extension. Calls `HubInstallResult`; raises `HubFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `HubExtension`, `HubInstallResult`, `tuple[HubExtension, ...]`, `tuple[HubSuggestion, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.host`, `tarel.hub.catalog`, `tarel.hub.contracts`, `tarel.hub.suggest`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.hub`, `tarel.hub`, `tarel.sdk.hub`

### `src/tarel/hub/catalog.py`

Load, enrich, and query the TAREL Hub catalog without importing plugin code.

- **Input:** parameters `extension_id: str`, `extensions: tuple[HubExtension, ...]`, `group: str`, `host_version: str`, `host_versions: str | None`, `hub_url: str | None`, `identifier: str`, `name: str`, `offline: bool`; reads environment variables; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `config_template_for_extension`: Return a minimal safe configuration or usage template for one Hub extension.
    - `load_hub_catalog`: Load the TAREL Hub catalog and enrich each entry with local host status. Raises `HubFailure`.
    - `load_bundled_hub_catalog`: Load the bundled offline Hub snapshot without touching the network.
    - `resolve_hub_extension`: Resolve an extension by id, package name, or entry-point name. Raises `HubFailure`.
    - `hub_hint_for_entry_point`: Return ``(extension_id, install_command)`` from the bundled snapshot, or ``None``.
    - `is_compatible_with_host`: Check simple PEP 440 range specifiers such as ``tarel>=0.0.6,<0.1``.
- **Output:** returns `HubExtension`, `bool`, `str | None`, `tuple[HubExtension, ...]`, `tuple[str, str] | None`
- **Uses:** `tarel`, `tarel.hub.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.connectors.host`, `tarel.hub.application`, `tarel.lineage.analyzers`

- **Processing:**

    - `HubExtension.recommended_cli_install`: recommended cli install; returns `str | None`.
    - `HubExtension.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
    - `HubInstallResult`: Result of planning or executing a TAREL Hub package installation.
    - `HubInstallResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
    - `HubSuggestion`: A static, read-only project recommendation for a TAREL Hub extension.
    - `HubSuggestion.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
- **Output:** returns `HubEntryPoint`, `HubExtension`, `dict[str, Any]`, `dict[str, str]`, `str | None`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.hub`, `tarel.hub`, `tarel.hub.application`, `tarel.hub.catalog`, `tarel.hub.suggest`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.hub`
