## `tarel.lineage`

### `src/tarel/lineage/_runtime_parse.py`

Contract versions, limits and strict JSON readers for runtime lineage documents.

- **Input:** parameters `choices: frozenset[str]`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `expected: set[str]`, `items: list[tuple[str, Any]]`, `label: str`, `limit: int`, `minimum: int`, `optional: set[str] | None`, `value: object`
- **Processing:**
    - `unique_object`: unique object; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_fields`: require fields; returns `None`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_boolean`: require boolean; returns `bool`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_object`: require object; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_objects`: require objects; returns `list[dict[str, Any]]`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_text`: require text; returns `str`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_strings`: require strings; returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_integer`: require integer; returns `int`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_rate`: require rate; returns `float`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_choice`: require choice; returns `str`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_sha256`: require sha256; returns `str`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `require_safe_code`: require safe code; returns `str`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `bool`, `dict[str, Any]`, `float`, `int`, `list[dict[str, Any]]`, `str`, `tuple[str, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.runtime`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_inputs`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_logical`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_records`

### `src/tarel/lineage/analysis_application.py`

Definition analysis: derive lineage workfiles with a provider or the SQLGlot adapter.

- **Input:** parameters `analyzer: str`, `definition_references: tuple[str, ...]`, `dialect: str | None`, `limit: int | None`, `max_output_tokens: int | None`, `model: str | None`, `name: str`, `progress: Callable[[str], None] | None`, `provider_name: str | None`, `provider_name: str`, `reasoning_effort: str | None`, `retry: int` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `LineageProviderRunResult`: record with fields document, path, provider, model, planned, applied, cache_hits, provider_requests ….
    - `run_lineage_provider_use_case`: run lineage provider (use case); returns `LineageProviderRunResult`. Calls `load_lineage_input`, `plan_lineage_tasks`, `load_provider`, `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity`, `lineage_analyzer_version`, `record_lineage_analysis_failure` …; uses the lineage store; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `run_lineage_analysis_use_case`: Run the selected analyzer strategy through the shared validated apply path. Calls `load_lineage_input`, `plan_lineage_tasks`, `record_lineage_analysis_failure`, `apply_lineage_proposal`; uses the lineage store; raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `LineageProviderRunResult`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.analysis_cache`, `tarel.lineage.analyzers`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.core`, `tarel.lineage.source`, `tarel.lineage.tasks`, `tarel.providers.contracts`, `tarel.providers.host`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.lineage`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`

### `src/tarel/lineage/analysis_cache.py`

Content-addressed local cache for validated provider lineage workfiles.

- **Input:** parameters `analysis: dict[str, object]`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `identity: LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity`; loads stored documents; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity`: record with fields content_hash, definition_kind, language, analyzer_version, provider, model, review_passes, max_output_tokens ….
    - `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity.key`: key; returns `str`.
    - `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity.from_dict`: from dict; returns `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `FileLineageAnalysisCache`: class.
    - `FileLineageAnalysisCache.load`: load; returns `dict[str, object] | None`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `FileLineageAnalysisCache.save`: save; returns `Path`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `FileLineageAnalysisCache.path`: path; returns `Path`.
- **Output:** returns `LineageAnalysisCacheIdentity`, `Path`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, object] | None`, `str`; deletes files; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.analysis_application`, `tarel.runtime`

### `src/tarel/lineage/analyzers.py`

Extension point for external static SQL lineage analyzers (`tarel.lineage_analyzers`).

- **Input:** parameters `definition: SourceDefinition`, `dialect: str | None`, `name: str`; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `StaticLineageAnalysisResult`: Result returned by an installed static lineage analyzer plugin.
    - `StaticLineageAnalysisResult.complete`: complete; returns `bool`.
    - `LineageAnalyzer`: Contract implemented by static lineage analyzer plugins.
    - `LineageAnalyzer.name`: name; returns `str`.
    - `LineageAnalyzer.analyze`: analyze; returns `StaticLineageAnalysisResult`.
    - `list_lineage_analyzers`: List installed static lineage analyzer names without importing them.
    - `load_lineage_analyzer`: Load one installed static lineage analyzer by entry-point name. Raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `LineageAnalyzer`, `StaticLineageAnalysisResult`, `bool`, `str`, `tuple[str, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.hub.catalog`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.source`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.analysis_application`

### `src/tarel/lineage/application.py`

Lineage use cases: build, import, edit, review and trace static lineage documents.

- **Input:** parameters `catalog_map: Mapping[str, str] | None`, `claim_id: str`, `decision: str`, `description: str`, `evidence_reference: str`, `expected_revision: str | None`, `graph_names: tuple[str, ...]`, `job_name: str`, `job_reference: str`, `kind: str`, `language: str`, `limit: int` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `LineageChangeResult`: record with fields document, path, report, report_path.
    - `LineageReviewResult`: record with fields document, item, path.
    - `ManualJobResult`: record with fields document, definition, path.
    - `ManualHopResult`: record with fields document, item, path.
    - `build_lineage_use_case`: build lineage (use case); returns `LineageChangeResult`. Calls `load_lineage_input`.
    - `import_dbt_lineage_use_case`: import dbt lineage (use case); returns `LineageChangeResult`.
    - `load_lineage_use_case`: load lineage (use case); returns `LineageDocument`. Uses the lineage store.
    - `list_lineages_use_case`: list lineages (use case); returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Uses the lineage store.
    - `add_manual_job_use_case`: add manual job (use case); returns `ManualJobResult`. Calls `add_manual_job`, `create_manual_lineage`; uses the lineage store.
    - `add_manual_hop_use_case`: add manual hop (use case); returns `ManualHopResult`. Calls `add_manual_hop`; uses the lineage store.
    - `next_lineage_task_use_case`: next lineage task (use case); returns `LineageTask | None`. Calls `load_lineage_input`, `plan_lineage_tasks`; uses the lineage store.
    - `apply_lineage_proposal_use_case`: apply lineage proposal (use case); returns `LineageChangeResult`. Calls `load_lineage_input`, `apply_lineage_proposal`; uses the lineage store.
    - `list_lineage_items_use_case`: list lineage items (use case); returns `tuple[LineageReviewItem, ...]`. Calls `list_lineage_items`; uses the lineage store.
    - `decide_lineage_item_use_case`: decide lineage item (use case); returns `LineageReviewResult`. Calls `decide_lineage_item`; uses the lineage store.
    - `process_lineage_view_use_case`: process lineage view (use case); returns `tuple[ProcessStep, ...]`. Calls `process_view`; uses the lineage store.
    - `table_lineage_view_use_case`: table lineage view (use case); returns `tuple[TableLineage, ...]`. Calls `table_lineage`; uses the lineage store.
    - `lineage_status_use_case`: lineage status (use case); returns `LineageStatus`. Calls `lineage_status`; uses the lineage store.
    - `find_lineage_references_use_case`: find lineage references (use case); returns `tuple[LineageReference, ...]`. Calls `find_lineage_references`, `resolve_model_path`, `load_local_embedding_plugin`; uses the graph store.
    - `trace_upstream_use_case`: trace upstream (use case); returns `UpstreamTrace`. Calls `trace_upstream`; uses the graph store.
- **Output:** returns `LineageChangeResult`, `LineageDocument`, `LineageReviewResult`, `LineageStatus`, `LineageTask | None`, `ManualHopResult`, `ManualJobResult`, `UpstreamTrace`, `tuple[LineageReference, ...]`, `tuple[LineageReviewItem, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.core`, `tarel.lineage.dbt`, `tarel.lineage.manual`, `tarel.lineage.refresh`, `tarel.lineage.review`, `tarel.lineage.revision`, `tarel.lineage.source`, `tarel.lineage.status`, `tarel.lineage.tasks`, `tarel.lineage.traversal`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.plugins`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.lineage`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.sdk.grounding`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`, `tarel.sdk.view`, `tarel.ui.backend`

Atomic local persistence for revision-bound lineage change reports.

### `src/tarel/lineage/contracts.py`

Dependency-free persisted contracts for reviewed static lineage.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `document: LineageDocument`
- **Processing:**
    - `LineageFailure`: Raised when a lineage operation is rejected.
    - `LineageEvidence`: record with fields source, reference, reason, line_start, line_end.
    - `LineageEvidence.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageWriteSource`: record with fields target, role, via, evidence.
    - `LineageWriteSource.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageExcludedWrite`: record with fields operation, target, disposition, evidence.
    - `LineageExcludedWrite.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageReview`: record with fields decision, reason, source.
    - `LineageReview.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `LineageDefinition`: record with fields id, external_id, kind, name, qualified_name, language, source_reference, content_hash ….
    - `LineageDefinition.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `LineageStep`: record with fields id, external_id, name, definition_id, depends_on.
    - `LineageStep.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageAnalysis`: record with fields definition_id, definition_revision, summary, warnings, excluded_writes, analyzer, analyzer_version, dialect.
    - `LineageAnalysis.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageAnalysisFailure`: record with fields definition_id, definition_revision, code, provider, model.
    - `LineageAnalysisFailure.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageClaim`: record with fields id, definition_id, operation, target, state, evidence, reviews.
    - `LineageClaim.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageWriteUnit`: record with fields id, definition_id, operation, target, state, evidence, sources, warnings ….
    - `LineageWriteUnit.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageMaterialization`: record with fields id, definition_id, target, mode, state, evidence, reviews.
    - `LineageMaterialization.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageDocument`: record with fields name, source_kind, source_name, source_reference, source_revision, workflow_id, workflow_name, definitions ….
    - `LineageDocument.definition_by_id`: definition by id; returns `dict[str, LineageDefinition]`.
    - `LineageDocument.claim_by_id`: claim by id; returns `dict[str, LineageClaim]`.
    - `LineageDocument.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageDocument.from_dict`: from dict; returns `LineageDocument`.
    - `validate_lineage_document`: Raise ``LineageFailure`` unless every section of ``document`` is internally consistent.
- **Output:** returns `LineageDocument`, `dict[str, LineageClaim]`, `dict[str, LineageDefinition]`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.lineage`, `tarel.context.grounding`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.focus.core`, `tarel.lineage`, `tarel.lineage._runtime_parse`, `tarel.lineage.analysis_application`, `tarel.lineage.analysis_cache`, `tarel.lineage.analyzers`, `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.lineage.change_store`, `tarel.lineage.core`, `tarel.lineage.dbt`, `tarel.lineage.manual`, `tarel.lineage.refresh`, `tarel.lineage.resolution`, `tarel.lineage.review`, `tarel.lineage.revision`, `tarel.lineage.runtime`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_application`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_inputs`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_logical`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_records`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_store`, `tarel.lineage.source`, `tarel.lineage.status`, `tarel.lineage.store`, `tarel.lineage.tasks`, `tarel.lineage.traversal`, `tarel.packages.application`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.lazy_family_view`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`, `tarel.ui.payloads.lineage`, `tarel.ui.payloads.lineage_flows`, `tarel.ui.payloads.lineage_source`, `tarel.ui.server`

### `src/tarel/lineage/core.py`

Deterministic transformations for write-centred static lineage.

- **Input:** parameters `analyzer: str`, `analyzer_version: str | None`, `code: str`, `definition_id: str`, `dialect: str | None`, `document: LineageDocument`, `model: str | None`, `name: str`, `payload: dict[str, Any]`, `provider: str`, `source: LineageInput`
- **Processing:**
    - `ProcessCall`: record with fields target, state.
    - `ProcessCall.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `ProcessStep`: record with fields id, name, definition_id, definition, depends_on, calls.
    - `ProcessStep.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `TableLineage`: record with fields source, target, via_definition, state, write_unit_id, role, via, derivation.
    - `TableLineage.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `build_lineage`: build lineage; returns `LineageDocument`. Calls `LineageDocument`, `validate_lineage_document`, `LineageStep`, `LineageDefinition`, `LineageMaterialization`, `LineageClaim` ….
    - `apply_lineage_proposal`: apply lineage proposal; returns `LineageDocument`. Calls `require_current_source`, `validate_lineage_document`, `LineageAnalysis`, `lineage_task`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `record_lineage_analysis_failure`: record lineage analysis failure; returns `LineageDocument`. Calls `validate_lineage_document`; raises `LineageFailure`, `LineageAnalysisFailure`.
    - `process_view`: process view; returns `tuple[ProcessStep, ...]`.
    - `table_lineage`: table lineage; returns `tuple[TableLineage, ...]`.
- **Output:** returns `LineageDocument`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `tuple[ProcessStep, ...]`, `tuple[TableLineage, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.coverage`, `tarel.lineage.source`, `tarel.lineage.tasks`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.analysis_application`, `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.lineage.refresh`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`

### `src/tarel/lineage/coverage.py`

Dependency-free coverage guard for potential SQL write statements.

- **Input:** parameters `source: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `WriteMarker`: record with fields operation, line, text.
    - `WriteMarker.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `write_markers`: Locate possible writes without claiming their targets or data dependencies.
- **Output:** returns `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[WriteMarker, ...]`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.core`, `tarel.lineage.tasks`

Manifest v12 declarations translated into the existing lineage input contract.

- **Processing:**
    - `load_dbt_manifest`: Read declared dependencies, not execution success or inferred SQL accesses. Calls `LineageInput`, `SourceDefinition`, `SourceStep`, `SourceMaterialization`, `SourceObservation`; raises `LineageFailure`.

### `src/tarel/lineage/manual.py`

Human-authored lineage overlays without modifying imported source documents.

- **Input:** parameters `description: str`, `document: LineageDocument`, `evidence_reference: str`, `job_reference: str`, `kind: str`, `language: str`, `line_end: int`, `line_start: int`, `name: str`, `operation: str`, `qualified_name: str`, `reason: str` …
- **Processing:**
    - `create_manual_lineage`: create manual lineage; returns `LineageDocument`. Calls `LineageDocument`, `validate_lineage_document`, `stable_id`.
    - `add_manual_job`: add manual job; returns `tuple[LineageDocument, LineageDefinition]`. Calls `stable_id`, `LineageDefinition`, `LineageStep`, `LineageAnalysis`, `validate_lineage_document`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `add_manual_hop`: add manual hop; returns `tuple[LineageDocument, LineageWriteUnit]`. Calls `LineageEvidence`, `LineageWriteUnit`, `validate_lineage_document`, `stable_id`, `LineageWriteSource`; raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `LineageDocument`, `tuple[LineageDocument, LineageDefinition]`, `tuple[LineageDocument, LineageWriteUnit]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.source`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.application`

### `src/tarel/lineage/refresh.py`

Revision-aware reconciliation of workflow sources and reviewed lineage knowledge.

- **Input:** parameters `current: LineageDocument`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `discovered: LineageDocument`, `source: LineageInput`
- **Processing:**
    - `LineageChange`: record with fields kind, severity, entity_type, reference, before, after.
    - `LineageChange.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageChange.from_dict`: from dict; returns `LineageChange`.
    - `StaleLineageItem`: record with fields item_type, definition_id, reference, previous_state, present, reasons, item.
    - `StaleLineageItem.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `StaleLineageItem.from_dict`: from dict; returns `StaleLineageItem`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `LineageRefreshReport`: record with fields before_revision, after_revision, changes, stale_items, carried_analyses, carried_failures, carried_claims, carried_write_units ….
    - `LineageRefreshReport.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LineageRefreshReport.from_dict`: from dict; returns `LineageRefreshReport`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `refresh_lineage`: Rebuild lineage from ``source`` and carry reviewed semantics over where still valid. Calls `build_lineage`, `validate_lineage_document`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `classify_lineage_changes`: classify lineage changes; returns `tuple[LineageChange, ...]`.
- **Output:** returns `LineageChange`, `LineageRefreshReport`, `StaleLineageItem`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[LineageChange, ...]`, `tuple[LineageDocument, LineageRefreshReport]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.core`, `tarel.lineage.source`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.lineage.change_store`

### `src/tarel/lineage/resolution.py`

Which object a lineage reference means; shared by traversal and the browser's flow view.

- **Input:** parameters `aliases: tuple[str, ...]`, `ambiguous: bool`, `declared: frozenset[str]`, `definition: LineageDefinition`, `definition_nodes: Mapping[str, str]`, `document: LineageDocument`, `graph_catalogs: frozenset[str]`, `index: ReferenceIndex`, `node_id: str`, `reference: str`, `scope: LocalScope`, `sessions: Mapping[str, str]` …
- **Processing:**
    - `LocalScope`: How far an unresolved name reaches: a stable key and the words a reader sees.
    - `ReferenceTarget`: What resolution needs to know about one object or definition.
    - `ReferenceResolution`: One reference: a target, the candidates a reader would have to choose from, or neither.
    - `ReferenceResolution.unresolved`: unresolved; returns `bool`.
    - `ReferenceIndex`: The names references can match; each caller decides what an unresolved one becomes.
    - `ReferenceIndex.add`: add; returns `None`.
    - `resolve_reference`: resolve reference; returns `ReferenceResolution`.
    - `declared_catalogs`: The known graph catalogs this document names explicitly in a fully qualified reference.
    - `temporary_scope`: How far a name the SQL engine keeps local can reach, or None for any other name.
    - `session_roots`: Which definitions run as one: joined by a step dependency or by one calling the other.
    - `local_scope`: The scope of a name no object answers to, or None when it names a shared table.
    - `scoped_label`: How a reader tells two equally spelled names apart, such as ``#tmp · etl``.
    - `display_reference`: display reference; returns `str`.
    - `normalize_reference`: normalize reference; returns `str`.
- **Output:** returns `LocalScope | None`, `ReferenceResolution`, `bool`, `dict[str, str]`, `frozenset[str]`, `str`, `str | None`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.traversal`, `tarel.ui.payloads.lineage_flows`

- **Processing:**

- **Output:** returns `tuple[LineageDocument, LineageReviewItem]`, `tuple[LineageReviewItem, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`

### `src/tarel/lineage/runtime_inputs.py`

Runtime lineage attempts: what a harness reports about one executed query or analysis.

- **Input:** parameters `consumes: tuple[str, ...]`, `contract_version: str`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `label: str`, `required: bool`, `status: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `RuntimeResultEvidence`: record with fields columns, row_count, sha256, truncated.
    - `RuntimeResultEvidence.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeResultEvidence.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeResultEvidence`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_strings`, `require_integer`, `require_sha256`, `require_boolean`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimeSQLAttemptInput`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, source, dialect, statement_sha256, inputs, result ….
    - `RuntimeSQLAttemptInput.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeSQLAttemptInput.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeSQLAttemptInput`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `require_strings`, `require_safe_code`, `require_integer`, `require_text` …; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimeMongoAttemptInput`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, source, operation, request_sha256, inputs, result ….
    - `RuntimeMongoAttemptInput.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeMongoAttemptInput.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeMongoAttemptInput`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `require_strings`, `require_safe_code`, `require_integer`, `require_text` …; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimeExecutor`: record with fields plugin_id, plugin_version.
    - `RuntimeExecutor.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `RuntimeExecutor.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeExecutor`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_safe_code`.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisInputFrame`: record with fields call_id, alias, source, frame_sha256.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisInputFrame.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisInputFrame.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeAnalysisInputFrame`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_text`, `require_safe_code`, `require_sha256`.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisLimits`: record with fields input_row_limit, output_row_limit, timeout_ms.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisLimits.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, int]`.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisLimits.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeAnalysisLimits`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_integer`.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisEvidence`: record with fields grain, join_coverage, unmatched_counts, reconciliation_status, duration_ms, limits.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisEvidence.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeAnalysisEvidence.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeAnalysisEvidence`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_strings`, `require_choice`, `require_integer`, `require_rate`, `require_object` …; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimeFederatedQueryInput`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, statement_sha256, consumes, result, error_code, executor ….
    - `RuntimeFederatedQueryInput.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeFederatedQueryInput.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeFederatedQueryInput`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `require_strings`, `require_safe_code`, `require_integer`, `require_text` …; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimePythonAnalysisInput`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, code_sha256, consumes, executor, input_frames, analysis ….
    - `RuntimePythonAnalysisInput.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimePythonAnalysisInput.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimePythonAnalysisInput`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `require_strings`, `require_integer`, `require_text`, `require_sha256`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `analysis_result`: analysis result; returns `tuple[RuntimeResultEvidence | None, str | None]`. Calls `require_safe_code`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `analysis_metadata`: analysis metadata; returns `tuple[RuntimeExecutor | None, tuple[RuntimeAnalysisInputFrame, ...], RuntimeAnalysisEvidence | None]`. Calls `require_object`, `require_objects`; raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `RuntimeAnalysisEvidence`, `RuntimeAnalysisInputFrame`, `RuntimeAnalysisLimits`, `RuntimeExecutor`, `RuntimeFederatedQueryInput`, `RuntimeMongoAttemptInput`, `RuntimePythonAnalysisInput`, `RuntimeResultEvidence`, `RuntimeSQLAttemptInput`, `dict[str, int]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage._runtime_parse`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.runtime_application`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_logical`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_records`

### `src/tarel/lineage/runtime_logical.py`

Caller-observed logical operations; no executable plans or implied artifact approval.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`
- **Processing:**
    - `RuntimeLogicalArtifactReference`: record with fields kind, graph, id, revision.
    - `RuntimeLogicalArtifactReference.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `RuntimeLogicalArtifactReference.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeLogicalArtifactReference`.
    - `RuntimeLogicalOperation`: Identical input/stored shape: historical artifact claims need no live graph rewrite.
    - `RuntimeLogicalOperation.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeLogicalOperation.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeLogicalOperation`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `RuntimeLogicalArtifactReference`, `RuntimeLogicalOperation`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage._runtime_parse`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_inputs`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.runtime_application`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_projection`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_records`, `tarel.sdk`

Read-only browser-shaped runtime projection, separate from reusable static ETL lineage.

- **Processing:**
    - `browser_runtime_lineage`: browser runtime lineage; returns `dict[str, object]`. Calls `validate_runtime_lineage_document`.
- **Output:** returns `dict[str, object]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.runtime`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_logical`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_records`

### `src/tarel/lineage/runtime_records.py`

Runtime lineage records: reported events, the stored document and its traces.

- **Input:** parameters `contract_version: str`, `data: dict[str, Any]`
- **Processing:**
    - `RuntimeLineageInput`: record with fields run_id, graph_name, graph_revision, events, contract_version.
    - `RuntimeLineageInput.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeLineageInput.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeLineageInput`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_text`, `require_sha256`, `require_object`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimeInputReference`: record with fields node_id, reference, kind.
    - `RuntimeInputReference.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `RuntimeInputReference.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeInputReference`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_choice`, `require_text`.
    - `RuntimeSQLAttempt`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, source, dialect, statement_sha256, inputs, result ….
    - `RuntimeSQLAttempt.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeSQLAttempt.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeSQLAttempt`. Calls `require_objects`.
    - `RuntimeMongoAttempt`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, source, operation, request_sha256, inputs, result ….
    - `RuntimeMongoAttempt.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeMongoAttempt.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeMongoAttempt`. Calls `require_objects`.
    - `RuntimeFederatedQuery`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, statement_sha256, consumes, result, error_code, executor ….
    - `RuntimeFederatedQuery.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`. Calls `RuntimeFederatedQueryInput`.
    - `RuntimeFederatedQuery.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeFederatedQuery`.
    - `RuntimePythonAnalysis`: record with fields sequence, call_id, status, code_sha256, consumes, executor, input_frames, analysis ….
    - `RuntimePythonAnalysis.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`. Calls `RuntimePythonAnalysisInput`.
    - `RuntimePythonAnalysis.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimePythonAnalysis`.
    - `RuntimeLineageDocument`: record with fields name, run_id, graph_name, graph_revision, events, contract_version.
    - `RuntimeLineageDocument.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeLineageDocument.from_dict`: from dict; returns `RuntimeLineageDocument`. Calls `require_fields`, `require_object`, `require_text`, `require_sha256`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `RuntimeTraceCall`: record with fields call_id, sequence, kind, status, operation.
    - `RuntimeTraceCall.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `RuntimeDependency`: record with fields source_call_id, target_call_id.
    - `RuntimeDependency.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `RuntimeLineageTrace`: record with fields runtime_lineage, start_call_id, graph_name, graph_revision, calls, dependencies, origins.
    - `RuntimeLineageTrace.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
- **Output:** returns `RuntimeFederatedQuery`, `RuntimeInputReference`, `RuntimeLineageDocument`, `RuntimeLineageInput`, `RuntimeMongoAttempt`, `RuntimePythonAnalysis`, `RuntimeSQLAttempt`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage._runtime_parse`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_inputs`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_logical`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.runtime`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_application`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_projection`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_store`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`

### `src/tarel/lineage/runtime_store.py`

Atomic persistence for immutable runtime-lineage documents.

- **Input:** parameters `document: RuntimeLineageDocument`, `name: str`; reads files
- **Processing:**

    - `FileRuntimeLineageStore.create`: create; returns `Path`. Calls `validate_runtime_lineage_document`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `FileRuntimeLineageStore.load`: load; returns `RuntimeLineageDocument`. Raises `LineageFailure`.

### `src/tarel/lineage/source.py`

Strict input-only contract for workflow definitions and source code.

- **Processing:**

    - `SourceMaterialization`: record with fields definition_external_id, target, mode, source_reference.
    - `SourceObservation`: record with fields definition_external_id, operation, target, source_reference, reason, line_start, line_end.

    - `LineageInput.definition_by_id`: definition by id; returns `dict[str, SourceDefinition]`.
    - `LineageInput.definition_by_external_id`: definition by external id; returns `dict[str, SourceDefinition]`.
    - `load_lineage_input`: load lineage input; returns `LineageInput`. Raises `LineageFailure`.

- **Output:** returns `LineageInput`, `dict[str, SourceDefinition]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.lineage.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.lineage.analysis_application`, `tarel.lineage.analyzers`, `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.lineage.core`, `tarel.lineage.dbt`, `tarel.lineage.manual`, `tarel.lineage.refresh`, `tarel.lineage.tasks`

    - `LineageStatus.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.

### `src/tarel/lineage/store.py`

Atomic local JSON persistence for lineage documents.

- **Input:** parameters `document: LineageDocument`, `name: str`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `LineageStore`: class based on Protocol.

    - `LineageStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.

    - `FileLineageStore.save`: save; returns `Path`. Calls `validate_lineage_document`, `write_atomic`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `FileLineageStore.load`: load; returns `LineageDocument`. Raises `LineageFailure`.

    - `FileLineageStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileLineageStore.path`: path; returns `Path`. Raises `LineageFailure`.

- **Used by:** `tarel.runtime`

### `src/tarel/lineage/tasks.py`

Provider-neutral coding-agent tasks for write-centred lineage extraction.

- **Input:** parameters `definition: SourceDefinition`, `document: LineageDocument`, `source: LineageInput`
- **Processing:**

### `src/tarel/lineage/traversal.py`

Deterministic upstream traversal across stored lineage and technical graphs.

- **Input:** parameters `documents: tuple[LineageDocument, ...]`, `embedder: EmbeddingBackend | None`, `graphs: tuple[GraphDocument, ...]`, `limit: int`, `max_hops: int`, `mode: str`, `query: str`, `reference: str`, `states: frozenset[str]`

    - `UpstreamTrace`: record with fields query, start, hops, origins, warnings, truncated.
    - `UpstreamTrace.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `find_lineage_references`: find lineage references; returns `tuple[LineageReference, ...]`. Calls `rank_bm25`; raises `LineageFailure`.
    - `trace_upstream`: trace upstream; returns `UpstreamTrace`. Raises `LineageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `UpstreamTrace`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[LineageReference, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.lineage.resolution`, `tarel.retrieval.bm25`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.context.grounding`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.focus.core`, `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.sdk.grounding`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`

## `tarel.logical_joins`

### `src/tarel/logical_joins/application.py`

Evidence-based promotion and policy-gated retrieval of logical join metadata.

- **Input:** parameters `candidate: DiscoveryCandidate`, `decision: str`, `endpoint: LogicalEndpoint | None`, `expected_revision: str`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_name: str | None`, `graph_name: str`, `join: LogicalJoin`, `join_id: str | None`, `join_id: str`, `limit: int`, `mode: str` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `LogicalJoinMatch`: record with fields join, endpoints.
    - `LogicalJoinMatch.usage`: usage; returns `str`.
    - `LogicalJoinMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `logical_join_summary`: logical join summary; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `promote_logical_join_use_case`: promote logical join (use case); returns `tuple[LogicalJoin, Path]`. Calls `LogicalJoin`, `physical_graph_revision`; uses the logical join store; raises `LogicalJoinFailure`.
    - `load_logical_join_use_case`: Load an audit record; find/review revalidate current dependencies. Uses the logical join store.
    - `list_logical_joins_use_case`: list logical joins (use case); returns `tuple[LogicalJoin, ...]`. Uses the logical join store.
    - `review_logical_join_use_case`: review logical join (use case); returns `LogicalJoin`. Uses the graph store, logical join store; raises `LogicalJoinFailure`.
    - `find_logical_joins_use_case`: find logical joins (use case); returns `tuple[LogicalJoinMatch, ...]`. Calls `physical_graph_revision`; uses the graph store; raises `LogicalJoinFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `LogicalJoin`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`, `tuple[LogicalJoin, ...]`, `tuple[LogicalJoin, Path]`, `tuple[LogicalJoinMatch, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.logical_program`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.logical_joins.contracts`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.logical_join`, `tarel.discovery.promotion`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.sdk.logical`, `tarel.ui.logical_metadata`

### `src/tarel/logical_joins/contracts.py`

Small promoted logical-join sidecars with existing aggregate Discovery evidence.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `include_revision: bool`, `value: object`
- **Processing:**
    - `LogicalJoinFailure`: Raised when a logical join operation is rejected.
    - `identifier`: identifier; returns `str`.
    - `sha256`: sha256; returns `str`.
    - `LogicalJoin`: record with fields id, graph_name, graph_revision, program, observations, run_id, run_revision, discovery_candidate_id ….
    - `LogicalJoin.revision`: revision; returns `str`.
    - `LogicalJoin.usage`: usage; returns `str`.
    - `LogicalJoin.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LogicalJoin.from_dict`: from dict; returns `LogicalJoin`.
- **Output:** returns `LogicalJoin`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.contracts`, `tarel.discovery.logical_program`, `tarel.discovery.runs`, `tarel.errors`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.logical_join`, `tarel.discovery.promotion`, `tarel.logical_joins.application`, `tarel.logical_joins.store`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.logical`, `tarel.ui.server`

### `src/tarel/logical_joins/store.py`

Atomic local sidecars for optional logical joins.

- **Input:** parameters `join: LogicalJoin`, `join_id: str`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `FileLogicalJoinStore`: class.
    - `FileLogicalJoinStore.path`: path; returns `Path`. Calls `identifier`; raises `LogicalJoinFailure`.
    - `FileLogicalJoinStore.exists`: exists; returns `bool`.
    - `FileLogicalJoinStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileLogicalJoinStore.load`: load; returns `LogicalJoin`. Raises `LogicalJoinFailure`.
    - `FileLogicalJoinStore.save`: save; returns `Path`. Calls `write_atomic`; raises `LogicalJoinFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `LogicalJoin`, `Path`, `bool`, `tuple[str, ...]`; replaces state files atomically
- **Uses:** `tarel.atomic`, `tarel.logical_joins.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.runtime`

### `src/tarel/object_bindings/application.py`

Protected value resolution shared by CLI/SDK; input values never enter TAREL storage.

    - `review_object_binding_use_case`: review object binding (use case); returns `ObjectValueBinding`. Raises `ObjectBindingFailure`.

- **Output:** returns `Mapping[str, object]`, `ObjectBindingResolution`, `ObjectValueBinding`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]`; replaces state files atomically

### `src/tarel/object_families/application.py`

Explicit, metadata-only object families shared by CLI, SDK and GUI.

- **Input:** parameters `allowed_object_ids: frozenset[str] | None`, `attributes: tuple[FamilyAttribute, ...]`, `decision: str`, `expected_revision: str`, `families: tuple[ObjectFamily, ...]`, `family: ObjectFamily`, `family_id: str`, `filters: Mapping[str, str] | None`, `grain: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_name: str`, `graphs: tuple[GraphDocument, ...]` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `FamilyProjection`: record with fields families, stale_graphs.
    - `FamilyMember`: record with fields object_id, reference, attributes.
    - `FamilyMember.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `FamilyMemberPage`: record with fields graph, family_id, revision, state, total_members, matched_members, offset, limit ….
    - `FamilyMemberPage.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `propose_object_family_use_case`: propose object family (use case); returns `ObjectFamily`. Calls `ObjectFamily`, `physical_graph_revision`; uses the graph store; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `validate_object_family_against_graph`: validate object family against graph; returns `None`.
    - `validate_object_families_against_graph`: Validate one graph-bound batch without rehashing or rescanning per family. Calls `physical_graph_revision`.
    - `import_object_family_use_case`: import object family (use case); returns `ObjectFamily`. Calls `validate_family`; uses the object family store, graph store; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `load_object_family_use_case`: Read an audit artifact. Use members/projection for current graph-bound use. Uses the object family store.
    - `review_object_family_use_case`: review object family (use case); returns `ObjectFamily`. Calls `review_family`; uses the graph store, object family store.
    - `family_summary`: Compact metadata; no member list, injected values or free-form producer/review notes.
    - `list_object_families_use_case`: list object families (use case); returns `tuple[dict[str, object], ...]`. Calls `physical_graph_revision`; uses the object family store, graph store.
    - `project_families_for_graphs_use_case`: project families for graphs (use case); returns `FamilyProjection`. Calls `physical_graph_revision`; uses the object family store.
    - `resolve_family_members_use_case`: resolve family members (use case); returns `FamilyMemberPage`. Raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `validate_family_selectively`: Check every physical member's exact schema without hydrating its fields. Calls `validate_family`, `physical_schema_revision`; uses the graph store; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `iter_family_metadata`: Stream physical names only; validate all declared affixes, including unselected members. Uses the graph store.
    - `physical_schemas`: physical schemas; returns `dict[str, tuple[FamilyField, ...]]`. Calls `FamilyField`; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `family_attribute_values`: family attribute values; returns `dict[str, str]`. Raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `require_family_mode`: require family mode; returns `None`. Raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `FamilyMemberPage`, `FamilyProjection`, `GraphHeader`, `Iterator[GraphNode]`, `ObjectFamily`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `dict[str, tuple[FamilyField, ...]]`, `tuple[dict[str, object], ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.graph.selective`, `tarel.object_families.contracts`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.family`, `tarel.context.hints_application`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.object_bindings.application`, `tarel.object_families.proposals`, `tarel.object_families.search`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.families`, `tarel.sdk.view`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.lazy_family_view`, `tarel.ui.logical_metadata`, `tarel.ui.payloads.families`

### `src/tarel/object_families/contracts.py`

Experimental, declarative families of explicitly named physical graph objects.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `decision: str`, `family: ObjectFamily`, `include_revision: bool`, `reason: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `ObjectFamilyFailure`: Raised when an object family operation is rejected.
    - `FamilyField`: A literal physical field name and schema, never an executable expression.
    - `FamilyField.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `FamilyField.from_dict`: from dict; returns `FamilyField`.
    - `FamilyAttribute`: A string attribute derived only by removing literal metadata affixes.
    - `FamilyAttribute.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `FamilyAttribute.from_dict`: from dict; returns `FamilyAttribute`.
    - `FamilyReview`: record with fields decision, reason, source.
    - `FamilyReview.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `FamilyReview.from_dict`: from dict; returns `FamilyReview`.
    - `ObjectFamily`: record with fields graph_name, graph_revision, id, name, member_ids, schema, grain, attributes ….
    - `ObjectFamily.revision`: revision; returns `str`.
    - `ObjectFamily.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ObjectFamily.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ObjectFamily`.
    - `validate_family`: Validate direct SDK construction as strictly as serialized imports.
    - `review_family`: review family; returns `ObjectFamily`.
- **Output:** returns `FamilyAttribute`, `FamilyField`, `FamilyReview`, `ObjectFamily`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.family`, `tarel.object_bindings.application`, `tarel.object_bindings.contracts`, `tarel.object_families`, `tarel.object_families.application`, `tarel.object_families.proposal_contracts`, `tarel.object_families.proposals`, `tarel.object_families.store`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.families`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`, `tarel.ui.lazy_family_view`, `tarel.ui.payloads.families`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`, `tarel.ui.server`

### `src/tarel/object_families/proposals.py`

Bounded internal LLM family suggestions; no data access, execution or auto-review.

- **Input:** parameters `graph_name: str`, `max_input_chars: int`, `max_objects: int`, `model: str | None`, `objects_per_batch: int`, `provider_name: str`, `resume: bool`, `run_id: str`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`, `timeout: float`, `workers: int`; loads stored documents; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `plan_family_proposals_use_case`: Checkpoint a bounded metadata inventory without making a generation request. Calls `identifier`, `physical_graph_revision`, `load_provider`, `FamilyProposalRun`, `physical_schemas`, `invalid` …; uses the object family store, graph store; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `load_family_proposals_use_case`: load family proposals (use case); returns `FamilyProposalRun`. Calls `invalid`; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `run_family_proposals_use_case`: Generate in bounded parallel batches; validate and save candidates serially. Calls `invalid`.
    - `_run_lock`: lock; returns `Iterator[None]`. Raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `FamilyProposalRun`; deletes files; replaces state files atomically
- **Uses:** `tarel.atomic`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.object_families.application`, `tarel.object_families.contracts`, `tarel.object_families.proposal_contracts`, `tarel.providers.contracts`, `tarel.providers.host`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.family_proposals`, `tarel.sdk.families`

## `tarel.packages`

### `src/tarel/packages/application.py`

Application use cases for portable ``.tarel`` metadata packages.

- **Input:** parameters `destination: Path`, `knowledge_ids: tuple[str, ...]`, `lineage_names: tuple[str, ...]`, `output: Path`, `path: Path`, `replace: bool`, `state_root: Path`, `workspace: str`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `pack_workspace`: Pack one workspace and its portable metadata into a ``.tarel`` file. Calls `package_report`, `write_bytes`, `write_manifest`, `entry_identity`, `write_document`, `PackageEntry`; raises `PackageFailure`.
    - `plan_workspace`: plan workspace; returns `PackagePlan`. Calls `PackagePlan`, `entry_identity`, `PackageEntry`, `sha256`.
    - `inspect_package`: Read the bounded manifest without expanding package documents. Calls `package_report`, `PackageArchive`, `read_manifest`, `validate_declared_members`.
    - `verify_package`: Verify archive safety, checksums, contracts, identities, and references. Calls `package_report`, `PackageArchive`.
    - `unpack_package`: Verify and extract into a new state root; merge and overwrite are not supported. Calls `PackageReport`, `PackageArchive`, `package_report`; raises `PackageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `PackagePlan`, `PackageReport`; deletes files; writes files; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.file_lock`, `tarel.focus.contracts`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.knowledge.contracts`, `tarel.knowledge.core`, `tarel.lineage.contracts`, `tarel.packages.archive`, `tarel.packages.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.package`, `tarel.packages`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`

### `src/tarel/packages/archive.py`

Bounded ZIP mechanics for portable TAREL packages.

- **Input:** parameters `archive: zipfile.ZipFile`, `compressed: bool`, `data: bytes`, `expected_size: int`, `label: str`, `manifest: PackageManifest`, `name: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `PackageArchive`: class.
    - `read_manifest`: read manifest; returns `PackageManifest`. Raises `PackageFailure`.
    - `validate_declared_members`: validate declared members; returns `None`. Raises `PackageFailure`.
    - `read_member`: read member; returns `bytes`. Raises `PackageFailure`.
    - `write_document`: write document; returns `tuple[str, int]`. Calls `sha256`; raises `PackageFailure`.
    - `write_manifest`: write manifest; returns `None`. Calls `pretty_json`.
    - `write_bytes`: write bytes; returns `None`.
    - `parse_json_object`: parse json object; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Raises `PackageFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `PackageManifest`, `bytes`, `dict[str, Any]`, `tuple[str, int]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.packages.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.packages.application`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`

Versioned contracts for portable TAREL metadata packages.

- **Input:** parameters `data: bytes`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `destination: Path | None`, `entries: tuple[PackageEntry, ...]`, `include_revision: bool`, `manifest: PackageManifest`, `name: str`, `path: Path`, `path: str`, `value: object`, `verified: bool`, `workspace: str`
- **Processing:**

- **Output:** returns `PackageEntry`, `PackageManifest`, `PackageReport`, `bytes`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`, `tuple[str, str]`

### `src/tarel/reference_mapping/contracts.py`

Strict, value-free contracts for directed reference mappings.

- **Input:** parameters `candidate: ReferenceMappingCandidate`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `decision: str`, `include_revision: bool`, `reason: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `ReferenceMappingFailure`: Raised when a reference mapping operation is rejected.
    - `ReferenceMappingEvidence`: record with fields phase, observation_id, level, query_hash, metrics, execution.

    - `ReferenceMappingProvenance`: record with fields run_id, run_revision, discovery_candidate_id, producer, promotion_reason, source_names.

    - `ReferenceMappingReview`: record with fields decision, reason, source.
    - `ReferenceMappingReview.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `ReferenceMappingReview.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ReferenceMappingReview`.
    - `ReferenceMappingCandidate`: record with fields id, graph_name, graph_revision, source_field_id, target_field_id, cardinality, mapping_manifest_hash, mapping_count ….
    - `ReferenceMappingCandidate.revision`: revision; returns `str`.
    - `ReferenceMappingCandidate.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ReferenceMappingCandidate.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ReferenceMappingCandidate`.

    - `ReferenceMappingMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `validate_reference_mapping_candidate`: validate reference mapping candidate; returns `None`.
    - `review_reference_mapping_candidate`: review reference mapping candidate; returns `ReferenceMappingCandidate`.
- **Output:** returns `ReferenceMappingCandidate`, `ReferenceMappingEvidence`, `ReferenceMappingProvenance`, `ReferenceMappingReview`, `bool`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `str`

Atomic private persistence for reference-mapping candidates.

## `tarel.relationships`

### `src/tarel/relationships/application.py`

Relationship use cases: add, check, discover and review join relationships in a graph.

- **Input:** parameters `config_path: Path | None`, `edge_id: str`, `expand_one_hop: bool`, `field_name: str | None`, `focus_name: str | None`, `from_reference: str`, `max_pairs: int`, `min_overlap_count: int`, `min_source_coverage: float`, `min_target_uniqueness: float`, `name: str`, `object_reference: str` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `RelationshipChangeResult`: record with fields graph, path, edge.
    - `RelationshipDiscoveryResult`: record with fields graph, path, profiles, candidates.
    - `add_relationship_use_case`: add relationship (use case); returns `RelationshipChangeResult`. Calls `relationship_pair`, `add_manual_relationship`, `graph_revision`; uses the graph store.
    - `check_relationship_use_case`: check relationship (use case); returns `RelationshipPairProfile`. Calls `relationship_pair`; uses the graph store.
    - `discover_relationships_use_case`: discover relationships (use case); returns `RelationshipDiscoveryResult`. Calls `candidate_pairs`, `add_profile_candidates`, `load_current_focus`, `graph_object_ids`, `expand_graph_objects_one_hop`, `graph_revision`; uses the graph store; raises `FocusFailure`.
    - `list_relationships_use_case`: list relationships (use case); returns `tuple[GraphEdge, ...]`. Calls `relationship_candidates`; uses the graph store.
    - `decide_relationship_use_case`: decide relationship (use case); returns `RelationshipChangeResult`. Calls `decide_relationship`, `graph_revision`; uses the graph store.
- **Output:** returns `RelationshipChangeResult`, `RelationshipDiscoveryResult`, `RelationshipPairProfile`, `tuple[GraphEdge, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.application`, `tarel.connectors.contracts`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.focus.contracts`, `tarel.focus.core`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.relationships.core`, `tarel.runtime`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.relationship`, `tarel.sdk.relationship`

### `src/tarel/relationships/core.py`

Small deterministic relationship operations over a TAREL graph.

- **Input:** parameters `allowed_object_ids: frozenset[str] | None`, `edge_id: str`, `field_name: str | None`, `from_reference: str`, `from_references: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `max_pairs: int`, `min_overlap_count: int`, `min_source_coverage: float`, `min_target_uniqueness: float`, `object_reference: str`, `origin: str` …
- **Processing:**
    - `RelationshipFailure`: Raised when a relationship operation is rejected.
    - `ResolvedField`: record with fields object_node, field_node.
    - `ResolvedField.reference`: reference; returns `str`.
    - `TransformedRelationshipProfile`: record with fields pair, pattern, component_index, component_start, component_length, pattern_sample_count, pattern_match_count, pattern_coverage ….
    - `TransformedRelationshipProfile.source_coverage`: source coverage; returns `float`.
    - `TransformedRelationshipProfile.target_uniqueness`: target uniqueness; returns `float`.
    - `TransformedRelationshipProfile.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `relationship_pair`: relationship pair; returns `RelationshipPair`.
    - `add_manual_relationship`: add manual relationship; returns `tuple[GraphDocument, GraphEdge]`.
    - `add_manual_relationship_fields`: Add one reviewable relationship whose ordered field pairs form one join. Calls `GraphEdge`.
    - `candidate_pairs`: candidate pairs; returns `tuple[RelationshipPair, ...]`.
    - `add_profile_candidates`: add profile candidates; returns `tuple[GraphDocument, tuple[GraphEdge, ...]]`.
    - `add_transformed_profile_candidates`: add transformed profile candidates; returns `tuple[GraphDocument, tuple[GraphEdge, ...]]`. Calls `GraphEdge`.
    - `relationship_candidates`: relationship candidates; returns `tuple[GraphEdge, ...]`.
    - `decide_relationship`: decide relationship; returns `tuple[GraphDocument, GraphEdge]`.
    - `usable_relationships`: usable relationships; returns `tuple[GraphEdge, ...]`.
    - `resolve_object`: resolve object; returns `GraphNode`.
    - `resolve_field`: resolve field; returns `ResolvedField`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphNode`, `RelationshipPair`, `ResolvedField`, `dict[str, object]`, `float`, `str`, `tuple[GraphDocument, GraphEdge]`, `tuple[GraphDocument, tuple[GraphEdge, ...]]`, `tuple[GraphEdge, ...]`, `tuple[RelationshipPair, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.contracts`, `tarel.errors`, `tarel.graph.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.discovery.checks`, `tarel.discovery.promotion`, `tarel.enrichment`, `tarel.entity_resolution.application`, `tarel.entity_resolution.discovery`, `tarel.reference_mapping.application`, `tarel.relationships.application`, `tarel.sources.application`, `tarel.ui.server`, `tarel.workspaces.core`

## `tarel.retrieval`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/application.py`

Retrieval use cases: embedding models and the local search indexes built from graphs.

- **Input:** parameters `_selection: _StatusSelection | None`, `annotation_states: frozenset[str] | None`, `areas: tuple[str, ...]`, `batch_size: int`, `force: bool`, `graphs: tuple[str, ...]`, `max_graphs: int`, `model_path: Path | None`, `model_sha256: str`, `n_threads: int | None`, `name: str`, `progress: Callable[[int, int, str], None] | None` …; loads stored documents; reads retrieval settings
- **Processing:**
    - `download_embedding_model_use_case`: download embedding model (use case); returns `ModelDownloadResult`. Calls `download_model`.
    - `embedding_model_status_use_case`: embedding model status (use case); returns `ModelStatus`. Calls `model_spec`, `sha256_file`, `default_model_path`.
    - `build_retrieval_index_use_case`: build retrieval index (use case); returns `IndexBuildResult`. Calls `snapshot_runtime`, `selected_annotation_states`, `local_embedding_input_strategy`; uses the retrieval index, graph store; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `retrieval_index_status_use_case`: retrieval index status (use case); returns `IndexStatus`. Calls `snapshot_runtime`, `selected_annotation_states`, `embedding_input_status`, `graph_revision`, `sqlite_vec_available`; uses the retrieval index, graph store.
    - `retrieval_workspace_status_use_case`: retrieval workspace status (use case); returns `WorkspaceIndexStatus`. Calls `snapshot_runtime`, `resolve_workspace_scope_use_case`, `GraphIndexStatus`, `sqlite_vec_available`.
    - `build_retrieval_workspace_indexes_use_case`: build retrieval workspace indexes (use case); returns `WorkspaceIndexBuild`. Calls `snapshot_runtime`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `embedding_backend`: embedding backend; returns `EmbeddingBackend`. Calls `HTTPEmbedding`, `load_settings`, `remote_identity`, `sha256_file`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `embedding_model_sha256`: embedding model sha256; returns `str`. Calls `remote_identity`, `sha256_file`, `load_settings`.
    - `resolved_embedding_model`: resolved embedding model; returns `Path | None`. Calls `selected_local_path`, `load_settings`, `resolve_model_path`.
- **Output:** returns `EmbeddingBackend`, `IndexBuildResult`, `IndexStatus`, `ModelDownloadResult`, `ModelStatus`, `Path | None`, `WorkspaceIndexBuild`, `WorkspaceIndexStatus`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.index`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.plugins`, `tarel.retrieval.remote`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.workspaces.application`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.index`, `tarel.cli.model`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.sdk.index`, `tarel.ui.backend`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/bm25.py`

Dependency-free BM25 used by local and hybrid retrieval.

- **Processing:**
    - `tokenize`: tokenize; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.

### `src/tarel/retrieval/catalog.py`

Task-specific model discovery; remote catalogs are fetched only on request.

- **Input:** parameters `provider: str`, `task: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `list_models`: List the models ``provider`` offers for ``task``; only remote providers use the network. Calls `HTTPRetrievalClient`, `default_model_path`, `installed_local_model_specs`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `ModelCatalog`
- **Uses:** `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.plugins`, `tarel.retrieval.remote`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/checkpoint.py`

Resumable index builds: embeddings are checkpointed in batches and reused when unchanged.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str]`, `destination: sqlite3.Connection`, `documents: tuple[RetrievalDocument, ...]`, `embedding_input: str`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `model_id: str`, `model_sha256: str`, `path: Path`, `rows: tuple[tuple[str, int, bytes], ...]`, `start: int`; opens database connections
- **Processing:**
    - `prepare_index_checkpoint`: prepare index checkpoint; returns `tuple[int, int | None]`. Calls `validated_vector_array`, `merge_vector_dimensions`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `copy_index_checkpoint_vectors`: copy index checkpoint vectors; returns `None`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `save_index_checkpoint_batch`: save index checkpoint batch; returns `None`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `tuple[int, int | None]`; deletes files; runs database queries; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.vectors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.retrieval.index`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/contracts.py`

Small contracts shared by local retrieval implementations.

- **Input:** parameters `batch_size: int`, `query: str`, `text: str`, `texts: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Processing:**
    - `RetrievalFailure`: Raised when a retrieval operation is rejected.
    - `RetrievalDocument`: record with fields id, object_id, field_id, namespace, label, text.
    - `RankedDocument`: record with fields document, score, sources.
    - `EmbeddingBackend`: The only model behavior required by TAREL retrieval.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.model_id`: model id; returns `str`.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.embed_documents`: embed documents; returns `tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]`.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.embed_query`: embed query; returns `tuple[float, ...]`.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.input_strategy`: Names how a text becomes model input; recorded so vectors stay comparable.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.max_input_tokens`: The per-text token limit, or None when the provider does not disclose it.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.token_count`: The tokens this text occupies, or None when only the provider can count them.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.embed_cut_document`: Embed the part of an overlong document that fits; the caller must record the cut.
    - `EmbeddingBackend.runtime_version`: The embedding library and version, or "" when TAREL cannot know it.
    - `RerankerBackend`: The only reranker behavior required by TAREL retrieval.
    - `RerankerBackend.score`: score; returns `tuple[float, ...]`.
    - `ModelSpec`: Declarative pinned model download link supplied by a retrieval plugin.
    - `ModelDownloadResult`: record with fields path, spec, reused.
    - `IndexMetadata`: record with fields contract_version, graph, graph_hash, document_count, dimensions, model_id, model_path, model_sha256 ….
    - `IndexMetadata.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `IndexBuildResult`: record with fields path, metadata, resumed_documents, reused_documents, embedded_documents, removed_documents.
    - `IndexChanges`: record with fields added_documents, changed_documents, removed_documents, unchanged_documents.
    - `IndexChanges.requires_embedding`: requires embedding; returns `int`.
    - `IndexChanges.current`: current; returns `bool`.
    - `IndexChanges.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, int | bool]`.
    - `ModelStatus`: Whether the local embedding model file exists and matches its published checksum.
    - `EmbeddingInputStatus`: What is known about how an index's vectors were produced.
    - `IndexStatus`: Whether one graph's index is current, complete and built with the selected model.
    - `GraphIndexStatus`: An :class:`IndexStatus` inside a workspace status, labelled with its graph.
    - `WorkspaceIndexStatus`: The combined index status of every graph in a workspace scope.
    - `IndexBuildSummary`: What one graph's index build embedded, reused and removed.
    - `WorkspaceIndexBuild`: One bounded batch of a workspace index update and the status after it.
    - `ModelEntry`: One selectable model; local entries also report installation, size and source.
    - `ModelCatalog`: The models a provider offers for one task (embedding or reranker).
- **Output:** returns `bool`, `dict[str, int | bool]`, `dict[str, object]`, `int`, `int | None`, `str`, `tuple[float, ...]`, `tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.retrieval`, `tarel.discovery.application`, `tarel.lineage.traversal`, `tarel.retrieval`, `tarel.retrieval.application`, `tarel.retrieval.bm25`, `tarel.retrieval.catalog`, `tarel.retrieval.checkpoint`, `tarel.retrieval.documents`, `tarel.retrieval.index`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.plugins`, `tarel.retrieval.ranking`, `tarel.retrieval.remote`, `tarel.retrieval.rerank`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.retrieval.vectors`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sdk.index`, `tarel.sdk.retrieval`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.server`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/documents.py`

Create safe retrieval documents from an in-memory graph.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str]`, `graph: GraphDocument`
- **Processing:**
    - `build_retrieval_documents`: Project only approved metadata; arbitrary metadata and evidence stay out. Calls `RetrievalDocument`.
- **Output:** returns `tuple[RetrievalDocument, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.retrieval.index`, `tarel.retrieval.ranking`, `tarel.retrieval.rerank`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/index.py`

The local SQLite retrieval index: one per graph and embedding model.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str]`, `backend: str`, `batch_size: int`, `countable: bool`, `current_input: str | None`, `embedder: EmbeddingBackend | None`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `limit: int`, `metadata: IndexMetadata`, `model_id: str | None`, `model_path: Path | None`, `model_sha256: str | None` …; loads stored documents; opens database connections
- **Processing:**
    - `vector_reuse_rule`: Decide which stored vectors a build keeps when the text itself is unchanged.
    - `embedding_input_status`: Report what the index can show about its vectors, without implying more.
    - `embedding_input_warning`: A search warning, only when vectors may not represent their whole document.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex`: class.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.build`: build; returns `IndexBuildResult`. Calls `build_retrieval_documents`, `IndexBuildResult`, `prepare_index_checkpoint`, `merge_vector_dimensions`, `IndexMetadata`, `copy_index_checkpoint_vectors` …; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.changes`: changes; returns `IndexChanges`. Calls `build_retrieval_documents`, `IndexChanges`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.metadata`: metadata; returns `IndexMetadata`. Calls `IndexMetadata`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.storage_complete`: storage complete; returns `bool`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.validate_selection`: validate selection; returns `IndexMetadata`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.load`: load; returns `tuple[IndexMetadata, tuple[RetrievalDocument, ...], tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]]`. Calls `RetrievalDocument`, `unpack_vector`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.rank`: rank; returns `tuple[RankedDocument, ...]`. Calls `rank_vectors`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.path`: path; returns `Path`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.checkpoint_path`: checkpoint path; returns `Path`.
    - `FileRetrievalIndex.checkpoint_status`: checkpoint status; returns `dict[str, object] | None`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `sqlite_vec_available`: sqlite vec available; returns `bool`.
- **Output:** returns `EmbeddingInputStatus`, `IndexBuildResult`, `IndexChanges`, `IndexMetadata`, `Path`, `bool`, `dict[str, object] | None`, `str`, `str | None`, `tuple[IndexMetadata, tuple[RetrievalDocument, ...], tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]]`; deletes files; runs database queries; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.retrieval.checkpoint`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.documents`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.remote`, `tarel.retrieval.vectors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.retrieval.application`, `tarel.retrieval.ranking`, `tarel.runtime`

Explicit model download and cache helpers for local retrieval Hub plugins.

- **Input:** parameters `force: bool`, `name: str`, `path: Path | None`, `path: Path`, `progress: Callable[[int, int], None] | None`, `target: Path | None`, `text: str`; reads environment variables
- **Processing:**
    - `qwen_embedding_query`: qwen embedding query; returns `str`.

### `src/tarel/retrieval/plugins.py`

Extension points for external local embedding and reranker plugins.

- **Processing:**

    - `load_local_reranker_plugin`: load local reranker plugin; returns `LocalRerankerPlugin`.

### `src/tarel/retrieval/ranking.py`

Hybrid ranking over a retrieval index: BM25, vectors and reciprocal rank fusion.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str]`, `bm25_weight: float | None`, `embedder: EmbeddingBackend | None`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `limit: int`, `mode: str`, `model_path: Path | None`, `model_sha256: str | None`, `namespace: str | None`, `object_ids: frozenset[str] | None`, `query: str`, `query_vector: tuple[float, ...] | None` …
- **Processing:**
    - `search_retrieval`: search retrieval; returns `SearchResults`. Calls `build_retrieval_documents`, `rank_bm25`, `embedding_input_warning`, `FileRetrievalIndex`; raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `validate_bm25_weight`: validate bm25 weight; returns `float`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `SearchResults`, `float`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.bm25`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.documents`, `tarel.retrieval.index`, `tarel.search`
- **Used by:** `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/remote.py`

Bounded standard-library adapters over configured HTTP provider profiles.

- **Input:** parameters `batch_size: int`, `choice: ModelChoice`, `count: int`, `key: str`, `payload: dict[str, object] | None`, `query: str`, `response: dict[str, object]`, `route: str`, `text: str`, `texts: tuple[str, ...]`, `value: object`
- **Processing:**
    - `HTTPRetrievalClient`: class.
    - `HTTPRetrievalClient.request`: request; returns `dict[str, object]`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `remote_identity`: remote identity; returns `str`. Calls `load_http_provider_config`.
    - `indexed_rows`: indexed rows; returns `list[dict[str, object]]`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `probability`: probability; returns `float`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding`: class.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.input_strategy`: input strategy; returns `str`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.max_input_tokens`: max input tokens; returns `None`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.runtime_version`: runtime version; returns `str`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.token_count`: token count; returns `None`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.embed_cut_document`: Never reached: without a known limit no document is marked for shortening. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.embed_documents`: embed documents; returns `tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
    - `HTTPEmbedding.embed_query`: embed query; returns `tuple[float, ...]`. Calls `qwen_embedding_query`.
    - `rerank_remote`: rerank remote; returns `tuple[float, ...]`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `dict[str, object]`, `float`, `list[dict[str, object]]`, `str`, `tuple[float, ...]`, `tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.providers.config`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`
- **Used by:** `tarel.retrieval.application`, `tarel.retrieval.catalog`, `tarel.retrieval.index`, `tarel.retrieval.rerank`

Optional relevance ordering of already scoped metadata objects.

- **Processing:**

- **Output:** returns `SearchResults`, `int`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.documents`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.plugins`, `tarel.retrieval.remote`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.search`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/search_application.py`

Search use cases: rank the objects of one graph or a workspace scope for a query.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str] | None`, `areas: tuple[str, ...]`, `bm25_weight: float | None`, `family_mode: str | None`, `filters: SearchFilters | None`, `focuses: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graphs: tuple[str, ...]`, `limit: int`, `mode: str`, `model_path: Path | None`, `n_threads: int | None` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `search_graph_use_case`: search graph (use case); returns `SearchResults`. Calls `snapshot_runtime`, `validate_bm25_weight`, `candidate_limit`, `selected_annotation_states`, `filter_search_objects`, `describe_search_results` …; uses the graph store; raises `SearchFailure`.
    - `search_workspace_use_case`: search workspace (use case); returns `SearchResults`. Calls `snapshot_runtime`, `validate_bm25_weight`, `candidate_limit`, `load_workspace_scope`, `selected_annotation_states`, `combine_workspace_search` …; uses the retrieval index; raises `SearchFailure`.
    - `project_graph_scope`: project graph scope; returns `GraphDocument`. Calls `GraphDocument`; raises `SearchFailure`.
    - `resolve_graph_object_scope`: Resolve explicit graph boundaries while preserving an intentionally empty scope. Calls `focus_object_allowlist`; raises `SearchFailure`.
    - `resolve_graph_object_scope_use_case`: Return every physical object allowed by one graph working scope. Uses the graph store.
- **Output:** returns `GraphDocument`, `SearchResults`, `tuple[str, ...]`, `tuple[tuple[str, ...], bool, tuple[str, ...]]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.object_families.search`, `tarel.retrieval.application`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.ranking`, `tarel.retrieval.rerank`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.search`, `tarel.workspaces.application`, `tarel.workspaces.retrieval`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.search`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.sdk.search`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`

### `src/tarel/retrieval/vectors.py`

Embedding vectors on disk: packing, validation and nearest-neighbour ranking.

- **Input:** parameters `candidate: int | None`, `code: str`, `connection: sqlite3.Connection`, `current: int | None`, `dimensions: int | None`, `dimensions: int`, `documents: tuple[RetrievalDocument, ...]`, `expected_count: int`, `indexed: tuple[tuple[RetrievalDocument, tuple[float, ...]], ...]`, `limit: int`, `query_vector: tuple[float, ...]`, `rows: tuple[tuple[str, int, bytes], ...]` …

    - `validate_vector_rows`: validate vector rows; returns `int`. Raises `RetrievalFailure`.

- **Output:** returns `array`, `bytes`, `dict[str, tuple[str, int, bytes]]`, `int`, `int | None`, `tuple[RankedDocument, ...]`, `tuple[float, ...]`; runs database queries
