## `tarel`

### `src/tarel/runtime.py`

Explicit local state boundary shared by the CLI and SDK.

- **Input:** parameters `factory: Callable[[], Any]`, `model_path: Path`, `model_sha256: str`, `n_threads: int | None`, `root: str | Path`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`
- **Processing:**
    - `TarelRuntime`: Filesystem-backed TAREL state rooted at one explicit ``.tarel`` directory.
    - `TarelRuntime.local`: local; returns `TarelRuntime`.
    - `TarelRuntime.graph_store`: graph store; returns `FileGraphStore`. Calls `FileGraphStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.graph_change_store`: graph change store; returns `FileGraphChangeStore`. Calls `FileGraphChangeStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.lineage_store`: lineage store; returns `FileLineageStore`. Calls `FileLineageStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.lineage_change_store`: lineage change store; returns `FileLineageChangeStore`. Calls `FileLineageChangeStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.lineage_analysis_cache`: lineage analysis cache; returns `FileLineageAnalysisCache`. Calls `FileLineageAnalysisCache`.
    - `TarelRuntime.runtime_lineage_store`: runtime lineage store; returns `FileRuntimeLineageStore`. Calls `FileRuntimeLineageStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.knowledge_store`: knowledge store; returns `FileKnowledgeStore`. Calls `FileKnowledgeStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.focus_store`: focus store; returns `FileFocusStore`. Calls `FileFocusStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.workspace_store`: workspace store; returns `FileWorkspaceStore`. Calls `FileWorkspaceStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.retrieval_index`: retrieval index; returns `FileRetrievalIndex`. Calls `FileRetrievalIndex`.
    - `TarelRuntime.embedding_backend`: Reuse one loaded local model for this long-lived SDK or UI runtime.
    - `TarelRuntime.model_sha256`: Hash a model once per unchanged file in this runtime.
    - `TarelRuntime.source_store`: source store; returns `FileSourceStore`. Calls `FileSourceStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.semantic_import_store`: semantic import store; returns `FileSemanticImportStore`. Calls `FileSemanticImportStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.entity_resolution_store`: entity resolution store; returns `FileEntityResolutionStore`. Calls `FileEntityResolutionStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.discovery_store`: discovery store; returns `FileDiscoveryStore`. Calls `FileDiscoveryStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.logical_topology_store`: logical topology store; returns `FileLogicalTopologyStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.reference_mapping_store`: reference mapping store; returns `FileReferenceMappingStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.logical_join_store`: logical join store; returns `FileLogicalJoinStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.semantic_concept_store`: semantic concept store; returns `FileSemanticConceptStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.sync_store`: sync store; returns `FileSyncStore`.
    - `TarelRuntime.object_family_store`: object family store; returns `FileObjectFamilyStore`.
    - `resolve_runtime`: The runtime a use case reads and writes with.
- **Output:** returns `Any`, `FileDiscoveryStore`, `FileEntityResolutionStore`, `FileFocusStore`, `FileGraphChangeStore`, `FileGraphStore`, `FileKnowledgeStore`, `FileLineageAnalysisCache`, `FileLineageChangeStore`, `FileLineageStore`
- **Uses:** `tarel.discovery.store`, `tarel.entity_resolution.store`, `tarel.focus.store`, `tarel.graph.change_store`, `tarel.graph.store`, `tarel.knowledge.store`, `tarel.lineage.analysis_cache`, `tarel.lineage.change_store`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_store`, `tarel.lineage.store`, `tarel.logical_joins.store`, `tarel.object_families.store`, `tarel.reference_mapping.store`, `tarel.retrieval.contracts`, `tarel.retrieval.index`, `tarel.retrieval.local`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.store`, `tarel.semantics.store`, `tarel.sources.store`, `tarel.sync.store`, `tarel.topology.store`, `tarel.workspaces.store`
- **Used by:** `tarel.annotations.application`, `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.cli.ui`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.context.hints_application`, `tarel.discovery.advice`, `tarel.discovery.application`, `tarel.discovery.checks`, `tarel.discovery.coverage_application`, `tarel.discovery.promotion`, `tarel.discovery.tasks`, `tarel.entity_resolution.application`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.graph.reads`, `tarel.knowledge.application`, `tarel.lineage.analysis_application`, `tarel.lineage.application`, `tarel.lineage.runtime_application`, `tarel.logical_joins.application`, `tarel.object_bindings.application`, `tarel.object_families.application`, `tarel.object_families.proposals`, `tarel.object_families.search`, `tarel.reference_mapping.application`, `tarel.relationships.application`, `tarel.retrieval.application`, `tarel.retrieval.rerank`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.retrieval.settings`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk._base`, `tarel.sdk._scope`, `tarel.sdk.client`, `tarel.sdk.retrieval`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.application`, `tarel.semantics.application`, `tarel.sources.application`, `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.impacts`, `tarel.sync.inbox`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`, `tarel.topology.application`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.lazy_family_view`, `tarel.ui.logical_metadata`, `tarel.ui.optional_metadata`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`, `tarel.ui.server`, `tarel.workspaces.application`

## `tarel`

### `src/tarel/search.py`

Deterministic lexical search over graph objects and their fields.

- **Input:** parameters `annotation_states: frozenset[str]`, `fields: list[GraphNode]`, `filters: SearchFilters | None`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `inventory: SearchInventory | None`, `limit: int`, `namespace: str | None`, `node: GraphNode`, `object_ids: frozenset[str] | None`, `query: str`, `results: SearchResults`, `source_graph: str | None` …
- **Processing:**
    - `SearchFailure`: Raised when a search operation is rejected.
    - `SearchFilters`: Small, mode-independent filters applied before retrieval ranking.
    - `SearchFilters.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, list[str]]`.
    - `SearchInventory`: record with fields objects_in_scope, objects_after_filters, types, roles.
    - `SearchInventory.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `FieldSearchHit`: record with fields id, label, score, reasons.
    - `FieldSearchHit.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `FamilySearchReference`: A metadata hit, never an executable table or a member expansion.
    - `FamilySearchReference.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `SearchHit`: record with fields id, label, type, score, matched_terms, reasons, fields, source_graph ….
    - `SearchHit.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `SearchResults`: record with fields graph, query, terms, hits, mode, workspace, graphs, scope_hash ….
    - `SearchResults.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `search_graph`: Rank tables and views using only persisted graph metadata.
    - `filter_search_objects`: Resolve physical candidates once so every retrieval mode sees the same inputs. Calls `annotation_is_visible`.
    - `describe_search_results`: Attach decision metadata without changing ranking or inventing explanations. Calls `annotation_is_visible`.
    - `score_object`: score object; returns `SearchHit | None`.
- **Output:** returns `SearchHit | None`, `SearchResults`, `dict[str, list[str]]`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[frozenset[str] | None, SearchInventory]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.annotations.states`, `tarel.errors`, `tarel.graph.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli._args`, `tarel.cli.search`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.object_families.search`, `tarel.retrieval.ranking`, `tarel.retrieval.rerank`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.search`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`, `tarel.ui.server`, `tarel.workspaces.retrieval`

## `tarel.semantic_concepts`

### `src/tarel/semantic_concepts/application.py`

Shared CLI/SDK concept import, review and conservative metadata retrieval.

- **Input:** parameters `allowed_object_ids: frozenset[str] | None`, `concept_id: str | None`, `concept_id: str`, `decision: str`, `document: SemanticConceptDocument`, `endpoint: LogicalEndpoint | None`, `expected_revision: str | None`, `expected_revision: str`, `graph_name: str`, `limit: int`, `mode: str`, `object_ids: frozenset[str] | None` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `SemanticConceptMatch`: record with fields graph_name, document_revision, concept, usage, resolved_bindings.
    - `SemanticConceptMatch.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `save_semantic_concepts_use_case`: save semantic concepts (use case); returns `SemanticConceptDocument`. Uses the semantic concept store; raises `SemanticConceptFailure`.
    - `load_semantic_concepts_use_case`: Load the audit artifact; use find for current, policy-filtered context. Uses the semantic concept store.
    - `review_semantic_concept_use_case`: review semantic concept (use case); returns `SemanticConceptDocument`. Calls `ConceptReview`; uses the semantic concept store; raises `SemanticConceptFailure`.
    - `find_semantic_concepts_use_case`: find semantic concepts (use case); returns `tuple[SemanticConceptMatch, ...]`. Uses the semantic concept store; raises `SemanticConceptFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `SemanticConceptDocument`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[SemanticConceptMatch, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.contracts`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.concept`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.sdk.logical`, `tarel.ui.logical_metadata`

- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`, `tarel.topology.endpoint_contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.concept`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.logical`, `tarel.semantic_concepts`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.application`, `tarel.semantic_concepts.store`, `tarel.ui.server`

### `src/tarel/semantic_concepts/store.py`

Atomic local semantic-concept sidecars with strict identity validation.

- **Processing:**

Read-only projections that keep source semantics distinct from TAREL claims.

- **Input:** parameters `documents: Iterable[SemanticImportDocument]`
- **Processing:**
    - `SemanticTarget`: Any imported semantic object that can be projected onto graph objects.
    - `SemanticTarget.id`: id; returns `str`.
    - `SemanticTarget.name`: name; returns `str`.
    - `SemanticTarget.source_reference`: source reference; returns `str`.

## `tarel.sources`

### `src/tarel/sources/application.py`

Application operations over logical sources and connector capabilities.

- **Input:** parameters `annotate_new_provider: str | None`, `annotation_model: str | None`, `annotation_timeout: float`, `annotation_workers: int`, `config_reference: str | None`, `connector: str`, `database: str | None`, `enrichment_permissions: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph_name: str`, `graphs: tuple[str, ...]`, `name: str`, `namespace: str | None` …; loads stored documents; reads environment variables
- **Processing:**
    - `SourceChangeResult`: record with fields source, path, created.
    - `SourceCheck`: record with fields source, connector, config_status.
    - `SourceCheck.available`: available; returns `bool`.
    - `SourceCheck.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `SourceEnrichmentResult`: record with fields workfile, persisted_candidates, graph_path.
    - `SourceEnrichmentResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `configure_source_use_case`: configure source (use case); returns `SourceChangeResult`. Calls `check_connector`, `create_source`; raises `SourceFailure`.
    - `list_sources_use_case`: list sources (use case); returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `load_source_use_case`: load source (use case); returns `SourceProfile`.
    - `check_source_use_case`: check source (use case); returns `SourceCheck`. Calls `check_connector`.
    - `probe_source_use_case`: probe source (use case); returns `ProbeResult`. Calls `probe_connector_use_case`.
    - `discover_source_use_case`: discover source (use case); returns `CatalogResult`. Calls `discover_catalog_use_case`.
    - `build_source_graph_use_case`: build source graph (use case); returns `GraphBuildResult`. Calls `build_graph_use_case`.
    - `refresh_source_graph_use_case`: refresh source graph (use case); returns `GraphRefreshResult`. Calls `load_graph_use_case`, `refresh_graph_use_case`; raises `SourceFailure`.
    - `enrich_source_use_case`: enrich source (use case); returns `SourceEnrichmentResult`. Calls `compile_enrichment_workfile`, `add_transformed_profile_candidates`, `FileGraphStore`, `graph_revision`, `profile_connector_use_case`, `sample_connector_use_case`; uses the graph store; raises `SourceFailure`, `EnrichmentFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `CatalogResult`, `GraphBuildResult`, `GraphRefreshResult`, `ProbeResult`, `SourceChangeResult`, `SourceCheck`, `SourceEnrichmentResult`, `SourceProfile`, `bool`, `dict[str, object]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.connectors.application`, `tarel.connectors.contracts`, `tarel.connectors.host`, `tarel.enrichment`, `tarel.graph.application`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.graph.store`, `tarel.relationships.core`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sources.contracts`, `tarel.sources.store`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.source`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.sources`

## `tarel`

### `src/tarel/sync/__init__.py`

One-way publication of approved graph metadata from sites to a central view.

- **Input:** none (constants or declarations only)
- **Processing:** module-level constants and imports only.
- **Output:** no return values or effects

## `tarel.sync`

### `src/tarel/sync/admin_application.py`

Use cases around a takeover: reading a file, granting trust, status and physical cleanup.

- **Input:** parameters `annotations: bool`, `fingerprint: str`, `graph_id: str`, `local_graph: str`, `max_age_hours: float | None`, `namespaces: tuple[str, ...]`, `not_after: str | None`, `path: Path`, `public_key: str`, `publisher_id: str`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`, `stream_id: str` …
- **Processing:**
    - `inspect_publication_use_case`: Read and verify a file without any state. A readable file is not yet a trusted one. Calls `PublicationReport`, `frozen_copy`, `open_publication`, `object_count`.
    - `add_trust_use_case`: Allow one stream to write one local graph. Only this local command can grant that. Uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `set_trust_scope_use_case`: Replace what a stream may publish. A file can ask for more; only this grants it. Uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `trust_key_use_case`: Accept one more key. Adding it before revoking the old one rotates without a gap. Calls `require_identity`, `normalize_public_key`, `TrustedPublisher`, `key_fingerprint`; uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `revoke_key_use_case`: Stop accepting a key here. It reaches other states only when someone delivers it there. Uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `sync_status_use_case`: Report every trusted origin, including one that never delivered anything. Calls `load_workspaces`; uses the sync store.
    - `prune_revisions_use_case`: Delete revisions the active state no longer needs. Calls `require_identity`, `PruneResult`; uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `PruneResult`, `PublicationReport`, `TrustEntry`, `TrustedKey`, `tuple[StreamStatus, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.export`, `tarel.sync.impacts`, `tarel.sync.results`, `tarel.sync.signing`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.sdk.sync`

### `src/tarel/sync/application.py`

Use cases of a central state; CLI, SDK and any transport call these.

- **Input:** parameters `accept_history_gap: bool`, `allow_unsigned_dev: bool`, `path: Path`, `plan_id: str | None`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `plan_publication_use_case`: Say what applying this file would do, without changing active state. Calls `frozen_copy`, `open_statement`, `trusted_entry`, `check_sender`; uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `apply_publication_use_case`: Activate one file. ``plan_id`` refuses the work if any basis of a reviewed plan moved. Calls `frozen_copy`, `open_statement`, `trusted_entry`, `ApplyResult`, `check_sender`; uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `recover_activations_use_case`: Finish every activation a stopped process committed but did not complete. Uses the sync store.
- **Output:** returns `ApplyResult`, `SyncPlan`, `tuple[str, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.observation`, `tarel.graph.refresh`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sync.authorization`, `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.export`, `tarel.sync.impacts`, `tarel.sync.merge`, `tarel.sync.results`, `tarel.sync.store`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.core`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.sdk.sync`, `tarel.sync.inbox`

### `src/tarel/sync/authorization.py`

Who sent a publication, and what that sender may publish. Local trust decides both.

- **Input:** parameters `allow_unsigned_dev: bool`, `entry: TrustEntry`, `publication: Publication`, `statement: SignedStatement`, `store: FileSyncStore`
- **Processing:**
    - `trusted_entry`: trusted entry; returns `TrustEntry`. Raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `check_sender`: Who sent this, then whether they may say it. A good signature grants no permission. Raises `SyncFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `TrustEntry`
- **Uses:** `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.signing`, `tarel.sync.store`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sync.application`

### `src/tarel/sync/contracts.py`

Versioned contracts of a publication, its stream and the central record of it.

- **Input:** parameters `data: bytes`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `data: object`, `label: str`, `prefix: str`, `value: object`, `value: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `SyncFailure`: Raised when a publication or central update is rejected.
    - `sha256_hex`: sha256 hex; returns `str`.
    - `stable_digest`: Digest of a value this process built itself; received documents are hashed as raw bytes.
    - `require_identity`: require identity; returns `str`.
    - `require_name`: require name; returns `str`.
    - `require_timestamp`: Accept UTC only. Basic and extended ISO spellings both pass, so compare via ``instant``.
    - `instant`: The moment a validated UTC timestamp names; text order differs between ISO spellings.
    - `ExportScope`: The approved part of a graph. Only what is listed here may leave the site.
    - `ExportScope.digest`: digest; returns `str`.
    - `ExportScope.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ExportScope.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ExportScope`.
    - `Publication`: The signed statement about one package. Parsed from exact bytes, never re-serialized.
    - `Publication.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `Publication.from_dict`: from dict; returns `Publication`.
    - `PublisherStream`: A site's own record of one stream. Its counter never moves backwards.
    - `PublisherStream.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `PublisherStream.from_dict`: from dict; returns `PublisherStream`.
    - `TrustedKey`: One signing key of a publisher, as far as this state has been told about it.
    - `TrustedKey.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `TrustedKey.from_dict`: from dict; returns `TrustedKey`.
    - `TrustedPublisher`: record with fields publisher_id, keys.
    - `TrustedPublisher.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `TrustedPublisher.from_dict`: from dict; returns `TrustedPublisher`.
    - `TrustEntry`: Central permission for one stream. Written locally; a package can never add or widen one.
    - `TrustEntry.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `TrustEntry.from_dict`: from dict; returns `TrustEntry`.
    - `PendingActivation`: The graph write a committed head still owes. A later run finishes it after a crash.
    - `PendingActivation.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `StreamHead`: The authoritative answer to which publication of a stream is active.
    - `StreamHead.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `StreamHead.from_dict`: from dict; returns `StreamHead`.
- **Output:** returns `ExportScope`, `Publication`, `PublisherStream`, `StreamHead`, `TrustEntry`, `TrustedKey`, `TrustedPublisher`, `datetime`, `dict[str, object]`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sdk.sync`, `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.authorization`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.export`, `tarel.sync.impacts`, `tarel.sync.inbox`, `tarel.sync.merge`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`, `tarel.sync.results`, `tarel.sync.signing`, `tarel.sync.store`

### `src/tarel/sync/envelope.py`

The ``.tarelpub`` file: an unchanged ``.tarel`` package plus a statement about it.

- **Input:** parameters `artifact_sha256: str`, `directory: Path`, `manifest: PackageManifest`, `package: Path`, `path: Path`, `publication: Publication`, `signature: bytes | None`, `statement: SignedStatement`, `statement: bytes`
- **Processing:**
    - `OpenedPublication`: A verified package and the exact statement bytes, read from a private copy.
    - `SignedStatement`: A file's statement and signature. Nothing of its package has been expanded yet.
    - `content_revision`: Identity of the transferred content, free of TAREL versions and ZIP implementations. Calls `sha256_hex`.
    - `statement_bytes`: statement bytes; returns `bytes`. Calls `pretty_json`.
    - `write_envelope`: write envelope; returns `None`. Calls `write_bytes`.
    - `frozen_copy`: Copy a delivered file to a private place and hash what was copied.
    - `open_statement`: Read only the statement and signature, so a sender is checked before its package is.
    - `open_content`: Expand and verify the package a checked statement describes.
    - `open_publication`: Both steps at once, for a file whose sender needs no check here.
    - `file_sha256`: file sha256; returns `str`.
- **Output:** returns `Iterator[tuple[Path, str]]`, `OpenedPublication`, `SignedStatement`, `bytes`, `str`; deletes files; writes temporary files
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.packages.application`, `tarel.packages.archive`, `tarel.packages.contracts`, `tarel.sync.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.authorization`, `tarel.sync.inbox`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`

### `src/tarel/sync/export.py`

The allowlist deciding what of a local graph may leave a site.

- **Input:** parameters `graph: GraphDocument`, `scope: ExportScope`
- **Processing:**
    - `export_graph`: Reduce ``graph`` to what ``scope`` approves. A listed namespace must actually exist.
    - `check_exported_graph`: Reject anything an honest export under ``scope`` could not contain.
    - `object_count`: object count; returns `int`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphDocument`, `int`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.build`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.sync.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`

### `src/tarel/sync/impacts.py`

What an activation took away, and which central statements that left without an object.

- **Input:** parameters `current: GraphDocument`, `data: dict[str, Any]`, `head: StreamHead | None`, `local_graph: str`, `merged: GraphDocument`, `previous: ActiveImpacts`, `publication_id: str`, `removals: tuple[dict[str, str], ...]`, `replacement: GraphDocument | None`, `report: GraphRefreshReport`, `restored: tuple[str, ...]`, `runtime: TarelRuntime` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `ActiveImpacts`: record with fields removals, retired_claims.
    - `ActiveImpacts.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ActiveImpacts.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ActiveImpacts`.
    - `ActiveImpacts.retired_annotations`: retired annotations; returns `dict[str, dict[str, Any]]`.
    - `scope_change`: What changed in what the site approves, or None when nothing did.
    - `next_impacts`: Carry the record forward: new removals join it, anything that came back leaves it.
    - `load_workspaces`: Every readable workspace. One that cannot be read holds no reference this can report. Uses the workspace store.
    - `dangling_for_graph`: Central references to ``local_graph`` that point at nothing, with why if that is known. Calls `referenced_graphs`, `dangling_references`; uses the graph store.
- **Output:** returns `ActiveImpacts`, `dict[str, Any] | None`, `dict[str, dict[str, Any]]`, `dict[str, object]`, `tuple[WorkspaceDocument, ...]`, `tuple[dict[str, str], ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.refresh`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.core`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.store`

### `src/tarel/sync/inbox.py`

A directory as a transport: whatever delivers files there, one pass takes them over.

- **Input:** parameters `allow_unsigned_dev: bool`, `directory: Path`, `min_age_seconds: float`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`
- **Processing:**
    - `process_inbox_use_case`: Run one pass. A scheduler, if any, belongs to whoever calls this. Calls `InboxResult`, `file_lock`, `recover_activations_use_case`; raises `SyncFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `InboxResult`; writes files
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`, `tarel.file_lock`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.results`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.sdk.sync`

### `src/tarel/sync/merge.py`

Pure rules: how a publication relates to history, and how it meets central knowledge.

- **Input:** parameters `accept_history_gap: bool`, `current: GraphDocument | None`, `head: StreamHead | None`, `incoming_base: GraphDocument`, `local_graph: str`, `previous_base: GraphDocument | None`, `publication: Publication`, `publication_id: str`, `published: GraphDocument`, `retired: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]] | None`
- **Processing:**
    - `classify`: Decide from sequence and history alone; a clock never settles which publication wins. Raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `base_materialization`: A publication as central state shows it when nothing central was added to it.
    - `Merged`: record with fields graph, report, restored.
    - `merge`: Adopt the site's observations and keep whatever central state changed itself. Calls `refresh_graph`, `graph_revision`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphDocument`, `Merged`, `tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.refresh`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.sync.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sync.application`

### `src/tarel/sync/publish_application.py`

Use cases of a publishing site: its identity, its streams and the files it writes.

- **Input:** parameters `annotations: bool`, `completeness: str | None`, `graph: str`, `name: str`, `namespaces: tuple[str, ...]`, `observed_at: str | None`, `output: Path`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`, `signing_key: Path | None`, `stream_name: str`, `technical_descriptions: bool`, `unsigned_dev: bool`; loads stored documents; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `init_publisher_use_case`: Give this site its identity once. Host, path and cloud play no part in it. Uses the sync store.
    - `create_stream_use_case`: Name one export of one graph. Overlapping exports are refused rather than arbitrated. Calls `PublisherStream`, `require_name`, `ExportScope`; uses the sync store, graph store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `update_stream_use_case`: Change what a stream approves. The new profile version makes the change visible centrally. Calls `ExportScope`; uses the sync store; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `publish_use_case`: Write one publication file. The counter moves first, so a number is never used twice. Calls `PublishResult`, `read_signing_identity`, `require_timestamp`, `export_graph`, `Publication`, `statement_bytes` …; uses the sync store, graph store; raises `SyncFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `PublishResult`, `PublisherStream`, `str`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.packages.application`, `tarel.packages.archive`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.sync.envelope`, `tarel.sync.export`, `tarel.sync.results`, `tarel.sync.signing`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.sdk.sync`

### `src/tarel/sync/results.py`

Typed results of the sync use cases, identical for CLI and SDK.

- **Input:** none (constants or declarations only)
- **Processing:**
    - `PublishResult`: record with fields path, publication_id, publication, artifact_sha256.
    - `PublishResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `PublicationReport`: What a file claims. Reading it says nothing yet about whether this state trusts it.
    - `PublicationReport.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `SyncPlan`: What applying one file would do. ``plan_id`` changes whenever a basis of the plan changes.
    - `SyncPlan.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `StreamStatus`: One origin's state, with source age, receipt and acceptance kept apart.
    - `StreamStatus.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ApplyResult`: record with fields plan, applied, head.
    - `ApplyResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `InboxItem`: What one pass did with one file. ``deferred`` files stay in place for the next pass.
    - `InboxItem.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `InboxResult`: record with fields items.
    - `InboxResult.rejected`: rejected; returns `int`.
    - `InboxResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `PruneResult`: Old revisions physically removed. The active view did not change.
    - `PruneResult.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
- **Output:** returns `dict[str, object]`, `int`
- **Uses:** `tarel.sync.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.sync`, `tarel.sdk.sync`, `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.inbox`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`

### `src/tarel/sync/signing.py`

Signatures through OpenSSH's ``ssh-keygen -Y``; TAREL implements no cryptography itself.

- **Input:** parameters `key_path: Path`, `public_key: str`, `signature: bytes`, `statement: bytes`, `text: str`; reads environment variables; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `normalize_public_key`: Keep type and key only. A comment or an option could change what the check means. Raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `key_fingerprint`: The same ``SHA256:...`` text OpenSSH shows for this key. Raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `read_signing_identity`: The path to sign with and its public key. Only the public file is ever read. Raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `sign_statement`: Sign exact bytes. The agent stays reachable, and a key that needs a prompt fails instead.
    - `verify_statement`: Raise unless ``signature`` over ``statement`` comes from exactly ``public_key``.
- **Output:** returns `bytes`, `str`, `tuple[Path, str]`; writes files
- **Uses:** `tarel.sync.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.authorization`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`

### `src/tarel/sync/store.py`

Files under ``<state>/sync``. Packages never select this directory, so it never leaves a site.

- **Input:** parameters `entries: tuple[TrustEntry, ...]`, `frozen: Path`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_ids: dict[str, str]`, `head: StreamHead`, `impacts: ActiveImpacts`, `name: str`, `older_than_seconds: float`, `publication_id: str`, `publisher_id: str`, `publishers: tuple[TrustedPublisher, ...]`, `status: dict[str, Any]` …; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `FileSyncStore`: class.
    - `FileSyncStore.stream_lock`: Serialize work on one stream. Other streams and central writers continue meanwhile. Calls `file_lock`; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `FileSyncStore.configuration_lock`: Serialize a site's counter and identities, and changes to central trust. Calls `file_lock`; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `FileSyncStore.publisher_id`: publisher id; returns `str | None`. Calls `require_identity`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_publisher_id`: save publisher id; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.graph_ids`: graph ids; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_graph_ids`: save graph ids; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.publisher_streams`: publisher streams; returns `tuple[PublisherStream, ...]`.
    - `FileSyncStore.publisher_stream`: publisher stream; returns `PublisherStream`. Raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_publisher_stream`: save publisher stream; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.trust_entries`: trust entries; returns `tuple[TrustEntry, ...]`.
    - `FileSyncStore.trusted_publishers`: trusted publishers; returns `tuple[TrustedPublisher, ...]`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_trust_entries`: save trust entries; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_trusted_publishers`: save trusted publishers; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.trust_digest`: trust digest; returns `str`. Calls `sha256_hex`.
    - `FileSyncStore.head`: head; returns `StreamHead | None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_head`: save head; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.status`: status; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_status`: save status; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.incoming_directory`: incoming directory; returns `Path`.
    - `FileSyncStore.clear_abandoned_incoming`: A killed process leaves its private copy behind. It was never active, so it can go.
    - `FileSyncStore.artifact_path`: artifact path; returns `Path`.
    - `FileSyncStore.retain_artifact`: retain artifact; returns `None`. Calls `sync_directory`; raises `SyncFailure`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_activation`: Keep the exact graph a head promises, so a crash can finish without recomputing it.
    - `FileSyncStore.load_activation`: load activation; returns `GraphDocument`.
    - `FileSyncStore.impacts`: impacts; returns `ActiveImpacts | None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.save_impacts`: save impacts; returns `None`.
    - `FileSyncStore.revisions`: revisions; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileSyncStore.revision_directory`: revision directory; returns `Path`.
- **Output:** returns `ActiveImpacts | None`, `GraphDocument`, `Iterator[None]`, `Path`, `PublisherStream`, `StreamHead | None`, `dict[str, Any]`, `dict[str, str]`, `int`, `str`; deletes files; replaces state files atomically
- **Uses:** `tarel.atomic`, `tarel.file_lock`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.sync.contracts`, `tarel.sync.impacts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.authorization`

## `tarel.topology`

### `src/tarel/topology/application.py`

Application boundary for graph-bound logical-topology documents.

- **Input:** parameters `decision: str`, `derived_relations: tuple[DerivedRelation, ...]`, `document: LogicalTopologyDocument`, `expected_revision: str | None`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_name: str`, `graph_names: tuple[str, ...] | None`, `graph_store: GraphStore | None`, `graphs: tuple[GraphDocument, ...]`, `reason: str`, `relation_id: str`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `LogicalTopologyProjection`: Current sidecars plus stale graph names for a fail-soft read projection.
    - `new_logical_topology_document`: new logical topology document; returns `LogicalTopologyDocument`. Calls `LogicalTopologyDocument`, `physical_graph_revision`.
    - `save_logical_topology_use_case`: save logical topology (use case); returns `LogicalTopologyDocument`. Calls `validate_logical_topology`; raises `LogicalTopologyFailure`.
    - `decide_derived_relation_use_case`: decide derived relation (use case); returns `LogicalTopologyDocument`. Calls `review_derived_relation`; raises `LogicalTopologyFailure`.
    - `load_logical_topology_use_case`: load logical topology (use case); returns `LogicalTopologyDocument`.
    - `list_logical_topologies_use_case`: list logical topologies (use case); returns `tuple[LogicalTopologyDocument, ...]`.
    - `list_logical_topologies_for_graphs_use_case`: list logical topologies for graphs (use case); returns `tuple[LogicalTopologyDocument, ...]`.
    - `project_logical_topologies_for_graphs_use_case`: Select current optional sidecars without making a stale one break the graph UI.
    - `validate_logical_topology_against_graph`: validate logical topology against graph; returns `None`. Calls `validate_logical_topology`, `physical_graph_revision`; raises `LogicalTopologyFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `LogicalTopologyDocument`, `LogicalTopologyProjection`, `tuple[LogicalTopologyDocument, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.graph.store`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.topology.contracts`, `tarel.topology.store`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.topology`, `tarel.context.hints_application`, `tarel.expansion.projections`, `tarel.sdk.logical`, `tarel.sdk.view`, `tarel.topology.endpoints`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.logical_metadata`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`

    - `ExecutorProvenance.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ExecutorProvenance`.
    - `DerivationEvidence`: record with fields id, level, plan_revision, input_count, output_count, error_count, input_manifest_sha256, output_manifest_sha256 ….
    - `DerivationEvidence.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.

    - `DerivedRelation`: record with fields id, name, source, steps, output_schema, grain, evidence, state ….
    - `DerivedRelation.plan_revision`: plan revision; returns `str`.
    - `DerivedRelation.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `DerivedRelation.from_dict`: from dict; returns `DerivedRelation`.
    - `LogicalTopologyDocument`: record with fields graph_name, graph_revision, derived_relations, contract_version.
    - `LogicalTopologyDocument.revision`: revision; returns `str`.
    - `LogicalTopologyDocument.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `LogicalTopologyDocument.from_dict`: from dict; returns `LogicalTopologyDocument`.
    - `validate_derived_relation`: validate derived relation; returns `None`.
    - `review_derived_relation`: review derived relation; returns `DerivedRelation`.
    - `validate_logical_topology`: validate logical topology; returns `None`.
- **Output:** returns `DerivationEvidence`, `DerivedRelation`, `DerivedRelationReview`, `DerivedStep`, `EndpointRef`, `ExecutorProvenance`, `ExplodeStep`, `ExtractStep`, `Grain`, `LogicalTopologyDocument`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.topology`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk.logical`, `tarel.topology`, `tarel.topology.application`, `tarel.topology.store`, `tarel.ui.payloads.families`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`, `tarel.ui.payloads.logical`, `tarel.ui.server`

Calls `optional_string`, `string_list`, `family_view_scope_revision_from_revisions`, `family_view_scope_revision`, `resolve_family_members_use_case`, `require_string` …; uses the graph store; raises `UIFailure`.

### `src/tarel/ui/payloads/families.py`

Object-family layer: families, their members and the scope revision a page belongs to.

- **Input:** parameters `allowed_ids: set[str]`, `allowed_objects: set[tuple[str, str]]`, `catalog: str`, `collapsed: set[tuple[str, str]]`, `documents: Iterable[FocusDocument]`, `entities: tuple[EntityResolutionMatch, ...]`, `families: tuple[ObjectFamily, ...]`, `family: ObjectFamily`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_name: str`, `graphs: Iterable[GraphDocument]`, `mappings: tuple[ReferenceMappingMatch, ...]` …
- **Processing:**
    - `object_family_payloads`: Collapse metadata only; a member edge never becomes a family-wide edge. Calls `physical_graph_revision`, `validate_object_families_against_graph`, `family_summary`; raises `ObjectFamilyFailure`.
    - `family_object_payload`: Render an already validated family without inventing physical-table semantics. Calls `browser_id`.
    - `compact_family_workspace_members`: compact family workspace members; returns `None`.
    - `family_view_scope_revision`: Bind member pages to the server-resolved physical and report/cube scope. Calls `graph_revision`.
    - `family_view_scope_revision_from_revisions`: Identical view identity from verified full-source headers, not subset hashes.
    - `compact_family_focus_selection`: Keep focus provenance without leaking or relabelling collapsed member hops. Calls `browser_id`.
- **Output:** returns `dict[str, Any]`, `str`, `tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], set[str]]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.entity_resolution.candidates`, `tarel.focus.contracts`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.graph.revision`, `tarel.object_families.application`, `tarel.object_families.contracts`, `tarel.reference_mapping.contracts`, `tarel.topology.contracts`, `tarel.ui.payloads._ids`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`
- **Used by:** `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.lazy_family_view`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`

The Lineage canvas as data flow: objects are nodes, processes sit on the edges between them.

    - `LineageFlowModel.payload`: payload; returns `dict[str, Any]`.
    - `LineageFlowModel.neighbourhood`: Walk outward breadth first, so the nearest edges survive the limit.
    - `LineageFlowModel.edge_detail`: edge detail; returns `dict[str, Any] | None`.
    - `browser_lineage_flows`: browser lineage flows; returns `dict[str, Any]`.

SQL excerpts for the lineage panel, read on demand from the definition's own source.

- **Processing:**

### `src/tarel/ui/query_tools.py`

Bounded read-only browser adapters for the existing search/context use cases.

- **Input:** parameters `payload: dict[str, Any]`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None`, `scope: UIQueryScope`, `scope_objects: tuple[str, ...]`; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `UIQueryFailure`: class based on RuntimeError.
    - `ScopeSelectors`: The workspace selectors every scoped use case accepts as keyword arguments.
    - `UIQueryScope`: Server-owned launch selection, never constructed from a browser request.
    - `UIQueryScope.selectors`: selectors; returns `ScopeSelectors`.
    - `query_scope_snapshot`: Expose exact source revisions without inventing a viewport selection contract. Calls `resolve_workspace_scope_use_case`, `canonical_hash`, `load_focus_use_case`, `load_workspace_use_case`; uses the graph store.
    - `search_metadata`: search metadata; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Calls `SearchFilters`, `search_workspace_use_case`, `search_graph_use_case`.
    - `preview_context`: preview context; returns `dict[str, Any]`. Calls `compile_workspace_context_prefix_use_case`, `compile_context_prefix_use_case`, `compile_workspace_context_use_case`, `compile_context_use_case`, `describe_context_use_case`, `compare_context_use_case`.
    - `preview_expansion`: Expand fields for physical objects already present in a validated context packet. Calls `expand_context_use_case`, `resolve_workspace_scope_use_case`, `ExpansionTarget`, `resolve_graph_object_scope_use_case`.
- **Output:** returns `ScopeSelectors`, `dict[str, Any]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.compiler`, `tarel.context.output`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.expansion.contracts`, `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.search`, `tarel.workspaces.application`
- **Used by:** `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.server`

## `tarel.workspaces`

### `src/tarel/workspaces/application.py`

Workspace use cases: systems, areas, zones, cross-graph relationships and scopes.

- **Input:** parameters `area_name: str`, `areas: tuple[str, ...]`, `description: str | None`, `focuses: tuple[str, ...]`, `graph_names: tuple[str, ...]`, `graphs: tuple[str, ...]`, `name: str`, `object_references: tuple[str, ...]`, `objects: tuple[str, ...]`, `reason: str`, `relationship_id: str`, `runtime: TarelRuntime | None` …; loads stored documents
- **Processing:**
    - `WorkspaceChangeResult`: record with fields workspace, path.
    - `WorkspaceRelationshipChangeResult`: record with fields workspace, path, relationship, previous_state.
    - `create_workspace_use_case`: create workspace (use case); returns `WorkspaceChangeResult`. Calls `create_workspace`; uses the workspace store; raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `list_workspaces_use_case`: list workspaces (use case); returns `tuple[str, ...]`. Uses the workspace store.
    - `load_workspace_use_case`: load workspace (use case); returns `WorkspaceDocument`. Uses the workspace store.
    - `resolve_workspace_scope_use_case`: resolve workspace scope (use case); returns `ResolvedScope`.
    - `load_workspace_scope`: load workspace scope; returns `tuple[WorkspaceDocument, dict[str, GraphDocument], ResolvedScope]`. Calls `resolve_scope`, `ScopeSelection`, `focus_object_allowlist`, `intersect_scope_objects`; uses the graph store, workspace store.
    - `load_workspace_scope_use_case`: Reuse the validated documents within one UI request, without a shared cache.
    - `define_workspace_system_use_case`: define workspace system (use case); returns `WorkspaceChangeResult`. Calls `define_system`; uses the workspace store, graph store.
    - `define_workspace_area_use_case`: define workspace area (use case); returns `WorkspaceChangeResult`. Calls `require_system`, `define_area`, `parse_schema_reference`; uses the workspace store, graph store.
    - `define_workspace_zone_use_case`: define workspace zone (use case); returns `WorkspaceChangeResult`. Calls `require_system`, `define_zone`; uses the workspace store, graph store.
    - `add_workspace_relationship_use_case`: add workspace relationship (use case); returns `WorkspaceRelationshipChangeResult`. Calls `add_workspace_relationship`; uses the workspace store, graph store.
    - `decide_workspace_relationship_use_case`: decide workspace relationship (use case); returns `WorkspaceRelationshipChangeResult`. Calls `decide_workspace_relationship`; uses the workspace store.
    - `show_workspace_zone_use_case`: show workspace zone (use case); returns `ResolvedZone`. Calls `require_system`, `resolve_zone`; uses the graph store, workspace store.
- **Output:** returns `ResolvedScope`, `ResolvedZone`, `WorkspaceChangeResult`, `WorkspaceDocument`, `WorkspaceRelationshipChangeResult`, `tuple[WorkspaceDocument, dict[str, GraphDocument], ResolvedScope]`, `tuple[str, ...]`; saves documents to a store
- **Uses:** `tarel.focus.application`, `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.runtime`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.core`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.workspace`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.retrieval.application`, `tarel.retrieval.search_application`, `tarel.sdk._scope`, `tarel.sdk.view`, `tarel.sdk.workspace`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.query_tools`

### `src/tarel/workspaces/contracts.py`

Versioned, dependency-free workspace contracts.

- **Input:** parameters `data: dict[str, Any]`, `workspace: WorkspaceDocument`
- **Processing:**
    - `WorkspaceFailure`: Raised when a workspace operation is rejected.
    - `SchemaReference`: record with fields graph, namespace.
    - `SchemaReference.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `SchemaReference.from_dict`: from dict; returns `SchemaReference`.
    - `ZoneMember`: record with fields graph, object_id.
    - `ZoneMember.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `ZoneMember.from_dict`: from dict; returns `ZoneMember`.
    - `WorkspaceRelationshipEndpoint`: record with fields graph, object_id, fields.
    - `WorkspaceRelationshipEndpoint.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `WorkspaceRelationshipEndpoint.from_dict`: from dict; returns `WorkspaceRelationshipEndpoint`.
    - `WorkspaceRelationship`: record with fields id, source, target, state, origin, reason.
    - `WorkspaceRelationship.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `WorkspaceRelationship.from_dict`: from dict; returns `WorkspaceRelationship`.
    - `Area`: record with fields name, schemas, description.
    - `Area.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `Area.from_dict`: from dict; returns `Area`.
    - `Zone`: record with fields name, members, description.
    - `Zone.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `Zone.from_dict`: from dict; returns `Zone`.
    - `WorkspaceSystem`: record with fields name, graphs, areas, zones, description.
    - `WorkspaceSystem.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `WorkspaceSystem.from_dict`: from dict; returns `WorkspaceSystem`.
    - `WorkspaceDocument`: record with fields name, systems, description, contract_version, relationships.
    - `WorkspaceDocument.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `WorkspaceDocument.from_dict`: from dict; returns `WorkspaceDocument`.
    - `validate_workspace`: validate workspace; returns `None`.
- **Output:** returns `Area`, `SchemaReference`, `WorkspaceDocument`, `WorkspaceRelationship`, `WorkspaceRelationshipEndpoint`, `WorkspaceSystem`, `Zone`, `ZoneMember`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.errors`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli._args`, `tarel.cli.workspace`, `tarel.knowledge.application`, `tarel.knowledge.core`, `tarel.packages.application`, `tarel.sdk._scope`, `tarel.sdk.grounding`, `tarel.sdk.workspace`, `tarel.sync.admin_application`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.impacts`, `tarel.sync.publish_application`, `tarel.ui.backend`, `tarel.ui.lazy_family_view`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`, `tarel.ui.server`, `tarel.workspaces`, `tarel.workspaces.application`, `tarel.workspaces.core`, `tarel.workspaces.impact`, `tarel.workspaces.projection`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`, `tarel.workspaces.store`

### `src/tarel/workspaces/core.py`

Pure workspace transformations and graph-reference validation.

- **Input:** parameters `description: str | None`, `endpoint: WorkspaceRelationshipEndpoint`, `graph: GraphDocument`, `graph_names: tuple[str, ...]`, `graphs: Mapping[str, GraphDocument]`, `name: str`, `object_references: tuple[str, ...]`, `reason: str`, `reference: str`, `relationship_id: str`, `schemas: tuple[SchemaReference, ...]`, `source_reference: str` …
- **Processing:**
    - `ResolvedZoneObject`: record with fields graph, area, namespace, name, label, type, object_id.
    - `ResolvedZoneObject.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `DanglingReference`: A central reference whose target a published graph no longer contains.
    - `DanglingReference.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, str]`.
    - `ResolvedZone`: record with fields workspace, system, zone, description, objects.
    - `ResolvedZone.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `create_workspace`: create workspace; returns `WorkspaceDocument`. Calls `WorkspaceDocument`, `validate_workspace`.
    - `define_system`: define system; returns `WorkspaceDocument`. Calls `WorkspaceSystem`, `validate_workspace`.
    - `define_area`: define area; returns `WorkspaceDocument`. Calls `Area`, `validate_workspace`.
    - `define_zone`: define zone; returns `WorkspaceDocument`. Calls `Zone`, `validate_workspace`.
    - `add_workspace_relationship`: Persist one explicit join whose endpoints may belong to different graphs. Calls `WorkspaceRelationship`, `validate_workspace`; raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `decide_workspace_relationship`: decide workspace relationship; returns `tuple[WorkspaceDocument, WorkspaceRelationship]`. Calls `validate_workspace`; raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `parse_schema_reference`: parse schema reference; returns `SchemaReference`. Calls `SchemaReference`; raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `require_system`: require system; returns `WorkspaceSystem`. Raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `resolve_zone`: resolve zone; returns `ResolvedZone`. Raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `validate_system_references`: validate system references; returns `None`. Raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `validate_workspace_references`: validate workspace references; returns `None`. Raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `dangling_references`: Everything central in ``workspace`` that points into a published graph at nothing.
    - `referenced_graphs`: The graphs of this workspace something central points into, without reading any of them.
    - `missing_fields`: missing fields; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
- **Output:** returns `ResolvedZone`, `SchemaReference`, `WorkspaceDocument`, `WorkspaceSystem`, `dict[str, object]`, `dict[str, str]`, `frozenset[str]`, `tuple[DanglingReference, ...]`, `tuple[WorkspaceDocument, WorkspaceRelationship]`, `tuple[str, ...]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.relationships.core`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.workspace`, `tarel.sdk.workspace`, `tarel.sync.application`, `tarel.sync.impacts`, `tarel.workspaces.application`, `tarel.workspaces.projection`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`

- **Processing:**

    - `WorkspaceChangeImpact.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `workspace_change_impacts`: workspace change impacts; returns `tuple[WorkspaceChangeImpact, ...]`.

### `src/tarel/workspaces/projection.py`

Deterministic in-memory graph projection for one resolved workspace scope.

- **Input:** parameters `graph_name: str`, `graphs: Mapping[str, GraphDocument]`, `node_id: str`, `scope: ResolvedScope`, `workspace: WorkspaceDocument`, `workspace_name: str`
- **Processing:**
    - `workspace_graph_name`: workspace graph name; returns `str`.
    - `scoped_node_id`: scoped node id; returns `str`.
    - `project_workspace_scope`: Combine selected graph fragments without changing their persisted documents. Calls `GraphDocument`, `GraphEdge`, `GraphNode`.
- **Output:** returns `GraphDocument`, `str`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.core`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`
- **Used by:** `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.context.grounding_application`, `tarel.context.hints_application`, `tarel.expansion.application`, `tarel.object_families.search`, `tarel.workspaces.retrieval`

### `src/tarel/workspaces/retrieval.py`

Pure aggregation of per-graph retrieval results for one workspace scope.

- **Input:** parameters `limit: int`, `mode: str`, `query: str`, `results: tuple[SearchResults, ...]`, `scope: ResolvedScope`
- **Processing:**
    - `combine_workspace_search`: combine workspace search; returns `SearchResults`. Calls `SearchResults`, `workspace_graph_name`, `SearchHit`, `scoped_node_id`, `FieldSearchHit`.
- **Output:** returns `SearchResults`
- **Uses:** `tarel.search`, `tarel.workspaces.projection`, `tarel.workspaces.scope`
- **Used by:** `tarel.retrieval.search_application`

### `src/tarel/workspaces/scope.py`

Resolve deterministic object scopes across workspace graphs.

- **Input:** parameters `allowed_by_graph: Mapping[str, frozenset[str]]`, `graphs: Mapping[str, GraphDocument]`, `scope: ResolvedScope`, `selection: ScopeSelection | None`, `warnings: tuple[str, ...]`, `workspace: WorkspaceDocument`
- **Processing:**
    - `ScopeSelection`: record with fields systems, graphs, areas, schemas, zones, focuses, objects.
    - `ScopeSelection.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, list[str]]`.
    - `ResolvedScopeObject`: record with fields graph, system, area, namespace, object_id, label, type, zones.
    - `ResolvedScopeObject.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `ResolvedScope`: record with fields workspace, selection, graph_names, objects, scope_hash, warnings.
    - `ResolvedScope.to_dict`: to dict; returns `dict[str, object]`.
    - `resolve_scope`: Resolve one workspace selection without querying or mutating its graphs. Calls `validate_system_references`; raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `intersect_scope_objects`: Apply one explicit object boundary without treating an empty set as unrestricted.
- **Output:** returns `ResolvedScope`, `dict[str, list[str]]`, `dict[str, object]`
- **Uses:** `tarel.graph.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`, `tarel.workspaces.core`
- **Used by:** `tarel.cli.workspace`, `tarel.context.application`, `tarel.sdk`, `tarel.sdk._scope`, `tarel.sdk.context`, `tarel.sdk.grounding`, `tarel.sdk.lineage`, `tarel.sdk.search`, `tarel.sdk.view`, `tarel.sdk.workspace`, `tarel.ui.payloads.families`, `tarel.ui.payloads.graph`, `tarel.workspaces`, `tarel.workspaces.application`, `tarel.workspaces.projection`, `tarel.workspaces.retrieval`

### `src/tarel/workspaces/store.py`

Atomic local JSON workspace store.

- **Input:** parameters `name: str`, `workspace: WorkspaceDocument`; reads files
- **Processing:**
    - `WorkspaceStore`: Whole-document persistence boundary for local or shared workspace stores.
    - `WorkspaceStore.save`: save; returns `Path | str | None`.
    - `WorkspaceStore.load`: load; returns `WorkspaceDocument`.
    - `WorkspaceStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileWorkspaceStore`: class.
    - `FileWorkspaceStore.save`: save; returns `Path`. Calls `validate_workspace`, `write_atomic`; raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `FileWorkspaceStore.load`: load; returns `WorkspaceDocument`. Raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
    - `FileWorkspaceStore.list`: list; returns `tuple[str, ...]`.
    - `FileWorkspaceStore.path`: path; returns `Path`. Raises `WorkspaceFailure`.
- **Output:** returns `Path`, `Path | str | None`, `WorkspaceDocument`, `tuple[str, ...]`; replaces state files atomically
- **Uses:** `tarel.atomic`, `tarel.workspaces.contracts`
- **Used by:** `tarel.runtime`
